### [1] [Open-Set Cattle Muzzle Identification: A Leakage-Controlled Benchmark and Evaluation Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28663)
*Lalit BC,Dharmendra Singh Chaudhary,Shovit Nepal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将“牛鼻纹识别”从传统封闭集识别重塑为开放集、基于图库的身份识别任务，提出泄漏控制的评测协议，并在两类嵌入模型上验证：混合CNN‑ViT度量学习模型与MegaDescriptor‑L基础模型。结果显示在理想（oracle）阈值下两者DI曲线指标极高，但在可部署的阈值标定下性能显著回落；增量建库无需重训即可保持高Rank‑1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实养殖场景需要在不接触动物的前提下进行可靠个体识别，以支撑防疫、免疫、繁育与保险；然而现有鼻纹识别多为封闭集假设，无法拒识未登记个体，且不支持不停机增量注册与可靠阈值设定，评测中还常存在数据泄漏与不规范标定，影响落地。

Method: 1) 将问题表述为开放集图库识别：支持未见个体的拒识与无需重训的增量入库；2) 提出泄漏控制评测协议：身份不交叠划分、按折重训、独立保留集阈值标定、重复样本核验去重与自助法置信区间；3) 评估两种嵌入：a) 混合CNN‑ViT度量学习模型；b) MegaDescriptor‑L基础模型；4) 比较理想阈值与可部署阈值下的检测‑识别率、FAR、以及随参考图像数量变化的Rank‑1表现。

Result: 在“理想阈值”下，混合模型在FAR=1e-1/1e-2/1e-3时的检测‑识别率分别为98.3%/96.4%/93.6%；MegaDescriptor‑L为99.3%/98.1%/96.1%。采用可部署阈值标定后，目标FAR=1%时，混合模型实际FAR=1.03%，MegaDescriptor‑L为2.44%，显示与oracle差距明显。增量入库时，单参考图Rank‑1>91%，8张参考图可达97.3%，且无需重训、不损害已有图库。

Conclusion: 开放集表述、严格的泄漏控制与独立阈值标定对动物生物识别的可靠落地至关重要；高质量嵌入与合理标定可在不重训的前提下实现可扩展、可部署的牛只鼻纹识别与增量注册评测框架，为面向部署的动物生物特征系统提供实践范式。

Abstract: Reliable individual cattle identification supports disease surveillance, vaccination records, breeding management, and livestock insurance. Although the bovine muzzle provides a stable, non-contact biometric, existing muzzle-recognition systems largely assume a closed set of enrolled animals, limiting their practical deployment. We reformulate cattle muzzle biometrics as an open-set, gallery-based identification problem that can reject previously unseen animals and support incremental enrollment without model retraining. We introduce a leakage-controlled evaluation protocol based on identity-disjoint splits, per-fold retraining, held-out threshold calibration, verified duplicate removal, and bootstrap confidence intervals. We evaluate the framework using two contrasting embedding configurations: a hybrid CNN-ViT metric-learning model and the MegaDescriptor-L foundation model. Under oracle threshold selection, the hybrid model achieves detection-and-identification rates of 98.3%, 96.4%, and 93.6% at target false-acceptance rates of 10^(-1), 10^(-2), and 10^(-3), respectively, while MegaDescriptor-L achieves 99.3%, 98.1%, and 96.1%. However, deployable threshold calibration reveals a substantial difference between oracle and calibrated performance: the hybrid model achieves a false-acceptance rate of 1.03% at a 1% target, whereas MegaDescriptor-L reaches 2.44%. Incremental enrollment further achieves Rank-1 accuracy above 91% with a single reference image and up to 97.3% with eight reference images, without retraining the model or degrading the existing gallery. These results demonstrate that threshold calibration, leakage control, and embedding quality are critical for reliable open-set cattle identification and provide a practical evaluation framework for deployment-oriented animal biometric systems.

</details>


### [2] [Understanding Temporal Semantic Stability in Open-Vocabulary UAV Perception through Metric 3D Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28665)
*Saurbh Singh Jamwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文关注无人机开放词汇分割在时间维度上的不稳定问题，利用三维度量融合将逐帧预测绑定到世界坐标体素，提出体素级评估框架，联合衡量最终一致性、语义信念漂移（SBD）、观测持久度（OP）与不确定性。实验证明：逐帧“语义闪烁”普遍存在；若缺少重复观测，汇总一致性会虚高；随观测持久度增加，不一致性上升但信念漂移下降。结论：评估长期语义可靠性必须结合观测持久度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇分割在无人机动态视角下常出现同一场景多次观测的语义标签不稳定（时序不一致/闪烁），现有评估多在图像/帧层面，忽视了世界坐标层面的持久一致性与重复观测支持度，导致稳定性被高估。作者希望建立与世界空间绑定的度量，分解不稳定来源，并量化证据累积对稳定性的影响。

Method: 通过度量三维融合（metric 3D fusion）将逐帧语义结果投影并融合到世界坐标体素网格，进行体素级关联与统计。提出评估框架：1) 最终语义一致性（aggregate agreement）；2) 语义信念漂移SBD（随时间标签分布的漂移程度）；3) 观测持久度OP（体素被重复观测的次数/强度）；4) 语义不确定性。采用不同体素分辨率、几何关联方式、时间采样密度进行稳健性测试，并对不同分割骨干网络进行对比。

Result: 在UAVid-3D数据上发现显著逐帧语义闪烁；当体素重复观测较少时，汇总世界空间一致性会被高估；按持久度分层后，重复出现的体素显示更强的语义分歧，但随着更多证据累积，SBD逐渐降低。上述趋势在两种分割骨干、不同体素分辨率、几何关联与时间采样设定下均保持一致。

Conclusion: 世界空间的观测持久度是评估长期语义可靠性的关键条件变量。仅看聚合一致性可能误判稳定性；必须将一致性与观测支持度联合解读。所提体素级框架能够揭示时序不稳定与证据累积的关系，为无人机长期感知评测提供更可靠指标。

Abstract: Recent open-vocabulary segmentation models have advanced semantic perception for UAVs, but predictions from moving aerial platforms can remain temporally inconsistent across repeated observations of the same physical scene. We investigate temporal semantic stability by associating frame-wise predictions with persistent world-space locations through metric 3D fusion. We introduce a voxel-level evaluation framework that jointly characterises final semantic agreement, Semantic Belief Drift (SBD), Observation Persistence (OP), and semantic uncertainty. Experiments on UAVid-3D reveal substantial frame-wise semantic flicker and show that high aggregate world-space agreement can overstate temporal stability when locations have limited repeated-observation support. Persistence-stratified analysis shows that recurrent voxels expose greater semantic disagreement, while belief drift decreases as additional evidence accumulates. This behaviour is observed across two segmentation backbones and remains consistent under variations in voxel resolution, geometric association, and temporal sampling density. Conditions that reduce world-space recurrence can increase apparent aggregate stability, demonstrating that semantic consistency must be interpreted together with observation support. Our findings highlight observation persistence as an essential conditioning variable for evaluating long-horizon semantic reliability.

</details>


### [3] [Improving Spatial-Temporal Reasoning in Video-Language Models with Structured Video Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28666)
*Sadegh Mohammadian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“结构化视频提示”，一种在推理时、无需训练的输入组织方法，通过在视频中加入轻量的空间与时间结构锚点，提升VLM在时空跟踪与区域定位相关任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-语言模型在需要跨时间跟踪事件与在空间中精确落地答案的任务上脆弱。作者认为部分问题源于推理时视觉证据的组织方式欠佳，而非纯粹的模型推理能力不足。

Method: 在不改动模型权重、解码流程、也不改变主比较中的问题提示词的前提下，于推理阶段对输入视频进行“结构化”增强：给视频添加轻量的空间结构（例如显式的区域/框/网格锚点）与时间结构（例如帧/片段级索引或时间线锚点），为模型提供跨时空组织证据的显式锚点；作为训练外、零改动模型的推理策略。

Result: 在两个互补的视频基准与两个开放视频-语言模型上评测，多个设置中性能提升，增益大小依模型与任务而异，显示结构化输入能在若干场景下改善表现。

Conclusion: VLM的失败不仅来自推理能力局限，也与推理时证据呈现方式相关。结构化视频提示为提升视频理解提供了简单且实用的途径。

Abstract: Video-language models (VLMs) remain brittle on tasks that require tracking events over time and grounding answers in specific spatial regions. We propose that part of this limitation can be addressed through better organization of visual evidence at inference time. We introduce structured video prompting, a training-free inference-time method that augments the input video with lightweight spatial structure and temporal structure, providing explicit anchors for organizing evidence across space and time without changing model weights or decoding and without altering the question prompt in the main comparison. We evaluate this approach on two complementary video benchmarks and two open video-language models. Across these settings, structured inputs improve performance in several cases, with gains varying by model and task. Our findings suggest that some failures of VLMs arise not only from reasoning capacity, but also from how video evidence is presented at inference time. These results highlight structured video prompting as a simple and practical direction for improving video understanding.

</details>


### [4] [MIRAGE-CAD: Construction-Mediated Multimodal Generation of Executable CAD Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28669)
*Jizong Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRAGE-CAD提出一个跨模态到共享构造表示的系统，通过显式“构造计划”接口生成可执行的Python CAD程序，并在OpenCASCADE中构建与导出STEP；在图像、文本、点云、STEP/B-Rep四种输入上均实现较高的可构建与导出成功率，并证明强重建不依赖文本化表示，显式计划主要用于可读与可测量的中间验证；同时强调可执行性、几何保真和参数响应性需分别评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从已观测到的对象恢复参数化CAD程序存在根本歧义：不同建模过程可得到相同几何。现有方法往往混淆程序可执行性、几何保真与参数可调性，且多依赖文本化表示或缺少可解释中间表征，跨多种输入模态的一致方法欠缺。

Method: 提出MIRAGE-CAD：将自然语言、渲染图、点云、STEP/B-Rep等输入映射到共享的构造表示；通过显式的构造计划（construction-plan）接口调解程序生成；使用Python CAD代码并由OpenCASCADE执行以生成实体与STEP导出。进行对照实验：1) 直接条件于连续表示的解码器也能强重建；2) 采用与计划曝光匹配的解码器在逐部件几何保真上无显著损失；评估上分别度量可执行有效性、几何保真与参数响应。

Result: 在每模态各2,500条保留查询上，无检索辅助推理时构建成功率55.4–70.0%，STEP导出成功率52.3–66.2%。显示强重建不依赖将构造表示文本化；计划式解码器在几何保真度上无检测到实质性损失。构造计划与参考构造的一致性对后续执行成功具有信息性。

Conclusion: 显式构造计划提供可读、可测量的中间层，有助于诊断与提高执行成功率，但强几何重建并不依赖文本表达；评估应将可执行性、几何保真和参数响应解耦。该框架在多模态输入上取得稳健表现，指向更可解释、可评测的CAD程序恢复。

Abstract: Recovering an executable parametric CAD program from an observed object is fundamentally ambiguous, because the same final geometry can result from different construction procedures. We study this problem from four types of input: natural-language descriptions, rendered images, point clouds, and STEP/B-Rep geometry. MIRAGE-CAD maps each input to a shared construction representation and mediates program generation through an explicit construction-plan interface. The resulting Python CAD code is executed by an OpenCASCADE kernel to build the solid and export it as STEP. On 2,500 held-out queries per modality, the system achieves 55.4-70.0% build success and 52.3-66.2% STEP export success without retrieval at inference. Controlled comparisons show that strong reconstruction does not depend on expressing the construction representation as text: a decoder conditioned directly on the continuous representation also reconstructs strongly, while an exposure-matched plan-based decoder shows no detected material loss in per-part geometric fidelity. The explicit plan instead provides a readable and separately measurable intermediate representation whose agreement with the reference construction is informative about downstream execution success. Finally, we show that executable validity, geometric fidelity, and parametric responsiveness can diverge substantially and should therefore be evaluated separately.

</details>


### [5] [Memory-Efficient Training-Free Acceleration of Diffusion Transformers with BaryCache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28670)
*Chengjie Lu,Tianchi Deng,Zhengqi He,Zhijian Gao,Huisi Wu,Xueliang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种对Diffusion Transformer（DiT）采样的训练免加速：用重心外推进行逐步预测，稳定且不增加显存，带来最高3.3倍端到端加速并维持感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT生成质量高但采样迭代昂贵；每个去噪步都需大矩阵运算。现有缓存法虽能减少重复算子，但需存储中间态、显著增加显存占用，限制推理批量。需要一种既不扩展显存、又能显著加速且保持质量的方案。

Method: 引入数值稳定的重心外推（barycentric extrapolation）作为前向预测器，对DiT每个去噪步进行逐步（stepwise）轨迹外推，避免类似龙格现象（Runge phenomenon）的振荡伪影；方法为训练免（无需微调），以较小内存开销替代缓存中间状态的加速策略。

Result: 在图像与视频生成的广泛实验中，相比基线DiT推理，达到最高3.30倍端到端采样加速；在内存/质量之间取得更优折中，感知质量基本保持。

Conclusion: 重心外推可作为DiT采样的有效、稳定、训练免加速器，在不增加显存的前提下显著缩短采样时间并维持高感知质量。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers achieve high-fidelity image and video generation, but their iterative sampling remains expensive, for each denoising step requires large matrix operations. Existing cache-based acceleration reduces redundant computation yet increases the VRAM footprint by storing intermediate states, which can directly constrain inference batch size. In this work, we propose a training-free acceleration method that performs stepwise forecasting for DiT sampling using a Barycentric Extrapolator. By leveraging barycentric extrapolation, our predictor is numerically stable and alleviates oscillatory artifacts analogous to the Runge phenomenon during forward forecasting. Across extensive experiments on both image and video generation, our approach provides a favorable trade-off between memory usage and perceptual quality, while delivering up to 3.30x end-to-end sampling speedup compared with baseline DiT inference.

</details>


### [6] [Measuring Similarity between Artistic and AI Generated Images using Siamese Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28671)
*Diego Castro Elvira,Navil Pineda Rugerio,Jesús García-Ramírez,Cecilia Reyes-Peña,Ricardo Ramos-Aguilar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过语义-视觉联合嵌入与三元组损失，量化AI生成图像与原作的相似度，在自建成对数据集上取得约99.4%的测试准确率，显示对潜在“抄袭”判别的强区分力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI艺术常被质疑“抄袭”或过度模仿既有风格与构图，需要可量化、可复现实证方法来评估AI图像与原作的相似度，以支撑版权与伦理讨论。

Method: 采用冻结的CLIP编码器构建Siamese网络，使用三元组损失优化余弦相似度；数据通过Stable Diffusion XL Refiner 1.0的image-to-image与定制提示词生成，辅以语义描述与BLIP-2字幕，形成原作-生成图成对样本；评估类间中心距δμ与分类准确率。

Result: 模型在训练集99.9%准确率，最佳配置在测试集达99.4%，并显示显著类间分离（δμ=0.677）；与文献中高达81%风格复现与90%视觉相似的报告相呼应。

Conclusion: 冻结CLIP的Siamese嵌入配合三元组损失能有效区分原作与AI生成图，提供量化判别工具，为AI艺术相似度与潜在侵权评估奠定技术基础。

Abstract: AI-generated art has sparked debates around potential plagiarism, as these images may closely resemble existing artworks. This research quantifies the similarity between original pieces and AI-generated counterparts, particularly those produced by the Stable Diffusion XL Refiner 1.0. We use Siamese Networks with frozen CLIP encoders and cosine similarity optimized through triplet loss. A dataset of paired original and generated images was built using image-to-image generation and custom prompts, enriched with semantic descriptors and BLIP-2 captions. Prior studies report up to 81\% style replication and 90\% visual similarity. Our results show high discriminative performance: training accuracy reached 99.9\%, and the best model configuration achieved 99.4\% test accuracy with strong inter-class separation ($δμ$ = 0.677), demonstrating the effectiveness of our semantic-visual embeddings.

</details>


### [7] [FrameScope: Temporal Data Valuation for Stream Active Learning in Autonomous Vehicle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28672)
*Yuheng Zhu,Man-Ki Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FrameScope：在车端基于时间扩展的NTK进行帧价值评估，只向云端请求高价值帧标签，实现高效连续学习，减少遗忘与带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶处于高度动态环境，静态模型难以覆盖新场景与长尾边缘案例，需要连续学习。然而车辆产生海量视频流，现有方法用启发式采样，忽略时间动态、易冗余或漏掉关键学习时刻，且云端全量上传带来带宽与成本压力。

Method: 提出FrameScope：将神经切线核（NTK）理论扩展到时间域，对流式视觉数据进行“时间感知”的数据价值评估；在车端本地进行原则化帧选择，只对被评为高价值的帧向云端“oracle”模型请求标签；不上传全量视频，从而实现低带宽、可扩展的连续学习管线。

Result: 在多种域移情境的实验中，FrameScope优于现有连续学习与采样基线，表现为更高的样本效率、显著降低灾难性遗忘，并在带宽使用上大幅减少。

Conclusion: 时间域的数据估值能够为自动驾驶的连续学习提供更可靠的帧选择策略：在车端估值、仅云端标注精选帧，实现性能、样本效率与带宽之间的良好权衡，具备可扩展性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles operate in dynamic, ever-changing environments where new scenarios and edge cases constantly emerge. As a result, static learning models are inadequate for ensuring safe and reliable operation. Continuous learning is essential for adapting to these evolving conditions and maintaining robust performance across diverse real-world settings. However, autonomous vehicles generate massive streams of visual data during operation, and existing continuous learning approaches typically rely on heuristic sampling methods that fail to capture temporal dynamics, often overlooking critical learning opportunities or selecting redundant frames. In this paper, we introduce FrameScope, a temporal data valuation framework for continuous learning in autonomous vehicles. FrameScope extends neural tangent kernel theory to temporal domains, enabling principled valuation of streaming visual data. Unlike cloud-centric methods that transmit all video data for processing, our approach performs principled, local frame selection on the vehicle and queries a cloud-based oracle model only for labels of those high-value frames. Extensive experiments across multiple domain shifts show that FrameScope consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving higher sample efficiency and significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting in autonomous vehicle perception. By valuing data on the vehicle and querying only labels for selected frames, FrameScope reduces bandwidth requirements, enabling scalable operation with a lightweight cloud labeling service.

</details>


### [8] [AdaptAV: Continuous Adaption of Vision Models for Autonomous Vehicles Using Cloud-based Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28673)
*Yuheng Zhu,Dhruva Ungrupulithaya,Boluo Ge,Man-Ki Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种云端持续重训车辆端小型视觉模型的系统：车端上传数据，云端用高精度“oracle”模型指导训练并下发更新，以在不牺牲实时性的前提下逐步提升感知精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车端受算力与时延约束，需用浅层、参数更少的模型以保证推理速度，但小模型泛化差，遇到新场景性能下降，亟需在不增加车端负担下持续提升模型精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 构建云-车协同学习框架：车辆持续上传感知数据到云端；云端利用充足算力与加速器运行高精度“oracle”模型生成伪标注/教师信号，指导对车载小模型进行再训练或蒸馏；定期将更新后的轻量模型通过网络回传至车辆部署；循环迭代实现持续学习。

Result: 随着时间推移，车辆端模型在不增加推理延迟和资源占用的情况下逐步提升对新场景的感知准确率；系统在推理速度与精度之间取得更优平衡。

Conclusion: 云端驱动的持续重训结合高精度教师模型可有效弥补车端小模型泛化不足，通过迭代更新实现长期性能提升，适合资源受限且需低时延的自动驾驶感知部署。

Abstract: Deploying vision perception models in autonomous vehicles requires that we prioritize inference speeds, resulting in a model with shallower architectures and lesser model parameters (i.e., more pruned). Such small models do not generalize well, which could result in poor performance when encountered with novel scenarios. We propose a system that overcomes this by continuously retraining the vision models on the cloud with data uploaded by vehicles. We leverage the abundant compute resources, including machine learning accelerators, of the cloud to run a highly-accurate oracle model that will guide the retraining process of the on-vehicle model. This newly trained model is transmitted to the vehicle over the network and is utilized by the vehicle for perceptions, leading to improved inference accuracy over time.

</details>


### [9] [Multi-exposure HDR Imaging: A Review of Pixel-level and Feature-level Reconstruction Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28674)
*Qian Tao,Wei Wang,Chaobing Zheng,Zhengguo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了多曝光HDR成像在动态场景中的鬼影问题与解决方案，围绕多曝光融合（MEF）与去鬼影两大主题，系统梳理从传统滤波到深度学习方法的对齐与融合策略、监督范式、数据集与评测指标，并总结关键设计原则与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景动态范围远超单次曝光成像能力，多曝光可覆盖更宽动态范围，但序列拍摄在运动下易引入鬼影与错位；现有方法分散且术语不一，缺乏按“对齐域与融合域”统一视角的细粒度分类和系统比较，研究者需要明确不同方法在鲁棒性、效率与可部署性上的权衡。

Method: 提出面向HDR/MEF与去鬼影的综述框架：1）按空间域与特征域回顾传统与数据驱动方法；2）对深度方法按“对齐+融合”域细分为像素域（显式对齐：光流、空间变换）与特征域（隐式对齐：可变形卷积、注意力、潜表示融合）；3）跨监督设定（全监督/弱监督/无监督/self-supervised）比较代表性工作；4）汇总公开数据集与客观指标（PSNR/SSIM/HDR-VDP/MEF-SSIM等）与主观评测适用性；5）归纳设计要点与瓶颈。

Result: 综述表明：显式像素级对齐在大位移与遮挡下易失效且计算开销高；特征域隐式对齐更鲁棒但可解释性与稳定性受限；融合方面，多尺度与注意力增强在细节保持与亮度一致性上具优势；监督充分的数据集匮乏导致跨域泛化受限。不同方法在定量指标与主观观感上各有优劣，任务目标（HDR重建vs感知质量）影响模型选择与训练目标。

Conclusion: 未来方向包括：1）统一的对齐-融合联合学习与不确定性建模；2）面向大运动与遮挡的时空一致性与显著性/遮挡显式建模；3）更真实的动态HDR数据集与物理感知指标；4）轻量化与移动端可部署方案；5）利用事件相机/短长曝光联合、辐射度物理先验与自监督学习以缓解数据匮乏与泛化问题。

Abstract: Multi-exposure is an efficient way to capture real-world high-dynamic-range (HDR) scenes. However, HDR imaging suffers from severe ghosting artifacts in dynamic scenes due to the temporal gap between sequential exposures. In this article, we categorize the literature on two important topics on HDR imaging: multi-exposure fusion (MEF) and ghost removal. Conventional filter-based and data-driven methods are studied in pixel space and feature space. For popular deep learning-based approaches, we provide a granular taxonomy based on their alignment and fusion domains: pixel-space methods, which typically employ explicit motion compensation such as optical flow or spatial transformers, and feature-space methods, which leverage implicit alignment through deformable convolutions, attention mechanisms, or latent representation merging. Representative works are compared across different supervision settings, and key design principles are summarized. In addition, this survey summarizes commonly used datasets and evaluation metrics, discussing their applicability under diverse output forms. Finally, major bottlenecks and promising directions for future research are outlined.

</details>


### [10] [Multi-Agent Self-Improving Reinforcement Learning for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28675)
*Mingwen Zhang,Jisheng Dang,Minqiang Yang,Bimei Wang,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出用冻结的验证器在训练阶段指导视频时序证据选择，结合强化学习式相对策略梯度与校准损失，在多类视频推理任务上实现零样本迁移并稳定提升IoU与相关性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推理（如带定位的VQA与时间定位）多用局部监督（边界回归/跨度生成），而验证器多在推理时重排序，训练阶段较少利用。作者想探究：冻结的验证器能否作为训练信号，稳定提升跨任务、跨数据集的证据选择质量与可迁移性。

Method: 构建多智能体框架：可训练的Grounder负责采样候选轨迹与证据片段；冻结的Verifier对查询条件下的片段打分。训练包含两部分：1）组内相对策略梯度（group-relative policy gradient），鼓励优于同输入同组对手的轨迹；2）引导式（bootstrapped）校准损失，将时间预测向验证器偏好的跨度靠拢。整个模型在源任务上训练，目标任务零样本评测，模型规模达20亿参数。

Result: 在不做目标数据集微调的设置下：在带定位的VQA基准上获得28.7% IoU与25.4%答案-证据对齐准确率；在时间定位基准上46.1% IoU；在长视频问答基准上54.1%。相对同规模强基线，增益不大但稳定，尤其在IoU与中等重叠召回等相关性指标上更明显。

Conclusion: 冻结验证器可作为有效训练信号改善证据选择，并能零样本迁移到多种视频推理任务；但严格边界精度仍偏弱。总体上，方法在相关性导向指标上带来稳定提升。

Abstract: Video reasoning tasks such as grounded video question answering and temporal grounding require selecting temporal evidence that supports the query. In many current training setups, temporal supervision is applied through local objectives such as boundary regression or span generation, while verification is used mainly to rerank candidate segments at inference time. We study whether a frozen verifier can also guide training. Our multi-agent framework couples a trainable \emph{Grounder} with a frozen \emph{Verifier}: the Grounder samples candidate trajectories and evidence segments, the Verifier assigns query-conditioned segment scores, a group-relative policy-gradient objective favors trajectories that outperform their within-input peers, and a bootstrapped calibration loss steers temporal predictions toward verifier-preferred spans. Trained on source tasks and evaluated without target-dataset fine-tuning, a two-billion-parameter instantiation transfers zero-shot across grounded question answering, temporal grounding, and long-video question answering, reaching 28.7\% intersection-over-union and 25.4\% answer-grounding accuracy on a grounded-question-answering benchmark, 46.1\% intersection-over-union on a temporal-grounding benchmark, and 54.1\% on a long-video question-answering benchmark. Relative to a strong same-scale baseline, the gains are modest but consistent, with the clearest improvements on relevance-oriented metrics such as intersection-over-union and moderate-overlap recall. Within the tested benchmarks and transfer setting, the results support frozen verification as a training signal for evidence selection, while showing that strict boundary precision remains comparatively weaker. Code and models are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MASIRL-E50C/

</details>


### [11] [Automated pipeline for herbarium label digitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28676)
*Hiba Abbad,Hanane Ariouat,Eva Perez Pimpare,Nicolas Turenne,Eric Chenin,Abderrazak Sebaa,Edi Prifti,Jean-Daniel Zucker,Youcef Sklab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HERBIOME提出一个端到端、可模块化的标本标签自动数字化流水线，结合检测、文本定位、识别与语义结构化，显著降低转录负担，并为多模态生物多样性AI构建高质量图文配对数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标本图像已大规模公开，但标签上的关键元数据（采集者、地点、日期、生态记录等）难以规模化获取，限制了生物多样性信息学研究与多模态AI所需的标本级图文语料构建。

Method: 构建HERBIOME流水线：1) YOLOv8用于组件检测；2) CRAFT Hezar进行词级文本定位；3) 微调TrOCR识别印刷+手写混合文本（训练数据由通用转写数据与标本特异数据混合）；4) 使用GPT-4o Mini将转写结果结构化为标准化语义字段。评估采用MWS与SMA双指标。

Result: TrOCR在混合数据训练下达到4.05–4.10% CER。端到端在450份法国产地标本上，MWS=0.614–0.618、SMA=0.440–0.445。混合训练提升语义准确性，随机采样提升表面相似度；分类学相关字段仍是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: HERBIOME可自动从复杂异质的标签中抽取结构化元数据，减轻人工转录负担，并能构建忠实反映标本个体性的图文对，为下一代多模态生物多样性AI打基础。

Abstract: Digitized herbarium collections, now comprising over 100 million freely accessible specimen images, have become a critical resource for addressing fundamental questions in ecology and evolutionary biology. Yet the rich metadata encoded in herbarium labels (collector identities, geographic localities, collection dates, and ecological observations) remains largely inaccessible at scale, constraining both biodiversity informatics and the construction of specimen-specific image-text corpora for multimodal AI. We present HERBIOME, a modular end-to-end pipeline for automated herbarium label digitization, integrating YOLOv8-based component detection, CRAFT Hezar word-level text localization, fine-tuned TrOCR for recognition of mixed handwritten and printed text, and GPT-4o Mini for semantic metadata structuring into standardized fields. TrOCR was trained on a multi-source dataset combining general transcription corpora (CREMMA-AN, PictoCatalogs) with herbarium-specific data (RéColNat), achieving a Character Error Rate of 4.05-4.10%. End-to-end evaluation on 450 French herbarium specimens, using a dual-metric framework of Maximum Window Similarity (MWS: 0.614-0.618) and Semantic Metadata Accuracy (SMA: 0.440-0.445), reveals that hybrid training strategies improve semantic fidelity while random sampling maximizes surface similarity, with taxonomic fields remaining the principal bottleneck. By automating the extraction of structured metadata from complex, heterogeneous labels, HERBIOME reduces transcription burden, enables the construction of paired image-text datasets that faithfully capture specimen individuality, which is a prerequisite for next-generation multimodal biodiversity AI systems.

</details>


### [12] [Evaluating Constrained Iterative Refinement for Scalable Vector Graphics Generation with Off-the-Shelf VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28678)
*Matthew Perlman,James Beetham,Niels Da Vitoria Lobo,Amrit Singh Bedi,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文探讨如何在无需重新训练的情况下，让现成的视觉语言模型（VLM）在推理阶段生成可编译的SVG，用受约束解码与迭代改写结合视觉反馈来提升质量；结果表明受约束解码显著提升编译成功率，但迭代自纠与视觉推理仍成短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型多针对栅格图像，忽视了在网页与可扩展图形中广泛使用的SVG矢量表示；重新训练/微调成本高，因此希望通过推理期方法评估并激发通用VLM的SVG生成潜力与边界。

Method: 提出并系统评估一个“受约束迭代改进”框架：1) 受约束解码（确保语法/结构合法，提升可编译性）；2) 结构化编辑（对SVG元素进行可控修改而非自由文本重写）；3) 视觉反馈闭环（渲染中间结果，引导后续修改）；在多种VLM与生成设置下统一评测。

Result: 跨模型与设置：受约束解码显著提高SVG编译成功率；但在多轮迭代中，模型在视觉推理与自我纠错方面表现不足，限制了通过迭代获得持续改进的幅度。

Conclusion: 推理期方法能一定程度上把通用VLM适配为SVG生成器，显示出可行性与潜力；然而当前VLM在视觉推理和自纠上的能力不足，成为进一步提升质量与鲁棒性的主要瓶颈，提示需要更强的反馈利用或训练期改进。

Abstract: Scalable Vector Graphics (SVGs) power much of the modern visual ecosystem, yet state-of-the-art generative models focus almost entirely on rasterized images. We explore whether inference-time methods can unlock SVG generation capabilities in off-the-shelf vision-language models (VLMs). We systematically evaluate a constrained iterative refinement harness that combines visual feedback, structured editing, and constrained decoding to characterize the capabilities and limitations of current VLMs for SVG generation. Across multiple VLMs and generation settings, we find that constrained decoding improves compilation success rates, while iterative refinement reveals a deficit in visual reasoning and self-correction. Our results highlight both the promise and current limitations of using inference-time methods to adapt general-purpose VLMs for SVG generation.

</details>


### [13] [Multi-Sensor Mapping of Vulnerable Urban Settlements Using SAR, Multispectral, and Hyperspectral Imagery: A Case Study in Córdoba, Argentina](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28680)
*Luigi Russo,Anabella Ferral,Silvia Liberata Ullo,Paolo Gamba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多源遥感深度学习框架，在阿根廷科尔多瓦进行“贫民区可能性”制图；比较SAR、MS、HS及多模态早/中/晚融合，晚融合并结合HS效果最佳；外部脆弱性图层验证部分“假阳性”实为更广泛脆弱区；热浪期地表温度更高，揭示局地增温。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正规住区外观异质、官方名录不完整，传统EO识别难；需要利用多源数据互补性与DL以更准确、可解释地识别与理解城市脆弱性。

Method: 以补丁级分类为核心任务；数据包括PlanetScope高分多光谱、COSMO-SkyMed SAR、中分辨率PRISMA高光谱；以ReNaBaP为标签，地理分块四折验证；构建SAR-only、MS-only、加入HS的版本，并系统比较早/中/晚融合；使用外部市政脆弱性图层评估官方多边形外的检测；在热浪期间进行地表温度分析。

Result: 晚融合+HS在分类性能与空间选择性上最优；PRISMA HS为MS与SAR提供互补光谱信息；许多看似假阳性与更广泛城市脆弱区重叠；ReNaBaP聚落在热浪期显著高于周边地表温度，呈现局地热增强。

Conclusion: 多传感器EO融合（特别是晚融合并结合HS）能有效支持官方贫民区制图，并帮助解释更广泛的城市脆弱性格局；热环境证据进一步印证其社会—环境意义。

Abstract: Informal settlements represent a major urban challenge in rapidly expanding cities, yet their identification from Earth Observation (EO) data remains difficult because of their heterogeneous appearance and incomplete official inventories. This work presents a multi-sensor deep learning (DL) framework for slum-likelihood mapping in Córdoba, Argentina, integrating high-resolution PlanetScope multispectral (MS) imagery, COSMO-SkyMed (CSK) Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) data, and medium-resolution PRISMA hyperspectral (HS) observations. The problem is formulated as a patch-level classification task using the official Registro Nacional de Barrios Populares (ReNaBaP) inventory as reference, and the models are evaluated through four geographically partitioned folds. SAR-only and MS-only baselines, their configurations with PRISMA HS support, and early fusion (EF), middle fusion (MF), and late fusion (LF) strategies are systematically compared. Results show that LF+HS provides the best overall balance between classification performance and spatial selectivity, while PRISMA contributes complementary spectral information alongside the higher-resolution MS and SAR representations. Beyond the standard evaluation against ReNaBaP, an external municipal vulnerability layer is used to interpret detections outside the official polygons, showing that several apparent false positives overlap broader vulnerable urban areas. Thermal analysis further shows that ReNaBaP settlements exhibit significantly higher surface temperatures than their immediate surroundings during a heatwave event, indicating localised surface-heat amplification. Taken together, these results suggest that multi-sensor EO fusion can support both the mapping of ReNaBaP settlements and the interpretation of broader urban vulnerability patterns.

</details>


### [14] [CARD: Calibration via Agreement in Reverse Diffusion for Out-of-Domain MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28681)
*Jiaheng Dai,Weidong Guo,Qingbiao Li,Jie Xu,Yi Guo,Yuanyuan Wang,Zeju Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CARD方法，利用分类扩散模型的“逆扩散一致性”作为可靠性证据，对分割模型进行像素级温度缩放校准，在不改变分割结果的前提下降低域移条件下的校准误差；在多MRI任务上显著优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后处理校准在域移下失效，因为它们依赖终端预测（logits/熵/增强响应），而域移恰好腐蚀终端预测，导致对不可靠区域的自信错误。需要一种独立于终端预测的可靠性证据来进行稳健校准。

Method: 引入基于分类扩散模型的证据：(1) 生成式形状先验：当外观受污染时，容量受限的扩散先验仍保持合理形状，和判别式主分割器的分歧可指示主模型错误；(2) 逆扩散的逐步类分布：沿逆扩散轨迹在每一步得到类别分布，将持续性分歧与瞬时噪声区分。将整个轨迹上的分歧进行时间聚合，学习一个将该聚合映射为每像素、对所有类别共享的温度场（不改标签只改置信度）的函数，形成CARD。与匹配的判别式对照方法比较，分歧与Dice相关性分别为0.788 vs 0.521。

Result: CARD在心脏、前列腺、脑MRI的多种域移场景下，相比每个设定中的最强基线，在49个对比中有45个降低了校准误差；聚合分歧与Dice的相关性显著高于判别式基线（0.788 vs 0.521）。

Conclusion: 在域移下，利用扩散模型逆过程中的一致性作为外部可靠性证据，可在不改变分割结果的情况下有效校准置信度。CARD稳定提升了多任务MRI分割的概率校准，优于现有后处理方法。

Abstract: Probability calibration aligns model confidence with predictive accuracy, enabling clinicians to identify unreliable segmentation regions. This alignment breaks down under domain shift, where artifacts and unseen protocols produce confident errors. Existing post-hoc methods adapt the correction at test time, conditioning on predictive entropy, the logit pattern, or augmentation response, but each proxy is read from the terminal prediction, the very quantity that shift corrupts. This motivates reliability evidence beyond the terminal prediction, which categorical diffusion provides in two ways. First, a generative shape prior keeps a capacity-limited reference intact when appearance is corrupted, so its disagreement with the primary segmentor highlights primary-model errors. Second, every reverse step yields a class distribution, separating persistent disagreement from transient discrepancy. Aggregated over the trajectory, this disagreement correlates with Dice at 0.788, against 0.521 for a matched discriminative control. We therefore propose CARD (Calibration via Agreement in Reverse Diffusion), which maps the temporal aggregate of this disagreement to a temperature field applied per pixel across all classes, so that confidence changes while the segmentation does not. Across cardiac, prostate and brain MRI shifts, CARD lowers calibration error in 45 of 49 comparisons against the strongest baseline in each setting.

</details>


### [15] [Distributed Semantic Segmentation With Improved Rate-Distortion Trade-Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28684)
*Danish Nazir,Timo Bartels,Thorsten Bagdonat,Tim Fingscheidt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出两种用于边云协同语义分割的“源编解码器”，在极低码率下仍维持更优率失真表现，并在ADE20K与Cityscapes上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式DNN感知系统（边端编码器+云端解码器）受传输码率约束，常借助通用/固定类型的源编码器，但这种绑定和架构局限在低码率下导致次优的率失真权衡。需要能在超低码率下仍保性能的新型源编解码方案。

Method: 设计两种新源编解码器，专为边云分割任务优化：在边端对中间特征进行任务感知的压缩（而非仅用标准图像/视频编解码），并与云端解码网络协同训练以最小化任务损失与码率，整体实现端到端的率-任务失真联合优化。

Result: 在分布式语义分割中，于ADE20K在<0.2 bpp、Cityscapes在~0.03 bpp的极低码率下，mIoU达到或超过现有方法，取得SOTA率失真表现。

Conclusion: 任务感知、端到端联合优化的源编解码器能在超低码率下显著优于传统基于标准编解码的方案，为分布式感知系统提供更优率失真权衡与可扩展性。

Abstract: Distributed deep neural networks (DNNs) for dense perception tasks such as semantic segmentation execute an encoder DNN on edge devices, and a decoder DNN typically on a large-scale cloud platform with a particular constraint on transmission bitrate. Recent works employ source codecs to enable bitrate-efficient transmission between the edge device and the cloud. However, as these approaches are typically bound to a particular type of source codec and alternative network architectures are often not explored, this results in a suboptimal rate-distortion (RD) trade-off in the low-bitrate regime. In this work, we propose two novel source codecs that \textit{enable extremely low bitrates, while improving RD performance}. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed source codecs by achieving state-of-the-art performance in distributed semantic segmentation at below 0.2 (0.03) bits per pixel, measured using the mean intersection-over-union metric on ADE20K (Cityscapes).

</details>


### [16] [Data Diversity, Not Frequency Invariance: A Controlled and Self-Audited Study of Compression-Robust Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28685)
*Abbas Aliyev,Samir Rustamov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文对“频域特征与压缩不变表示是提升压缩后深度伪造检测鲁棒性的关键”这一广泛假设给出受控否定：在FaceForensics++上，容量与增强匹配的基线EfficientNet-B0在各压缩等级均优于包含频域与对抗压缩不变性的CAFRL；鲁棒性主要来自真实编码多样性而非频域或对抗不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区普遍认为：视频压缩削弱伪造痕迹，需依赖频域（如DCT/FFT）与压缩不变表征来应对。但该假设多缺乏在统一容量、训练配方与数据增强控制下的严格对照验证。作者意在在受控、预注册协议下检验这一假设的有效性与可复现性。

Method: 提出CAFRL框架：1) 频域分支（块DCT与FFT相位）；2) 依据压缩级别的频带注意力；3) 通过梯度反转实现压缩不变对抗分支。同时设置容量与数据增强严格匹配的基线（EfficientNet-B0），并进行预注册实验：在FaceForensics++多压缩质量设置上评测，含自查与配方修复（修正四项对频域假设不利的缺陷），并比较真实H.264 CRF重编码与合成JPEG增强对鲁棒性的影响。

Result: 按预注册规格，基线在每个压缩级别均优于CAFRL；在CRF 40时AUC高3.66点（单种子、配对）。自查发现四项缺陷并在公平配方下重测后，差距归因于训练配方而非架构失败；基线配方比“出厂配方”恢复3.96点AUC。频域路径单独可判别（独立验证AUC 0.91–0.98），但与主干融合下无边际增益；不同宽度与种子池化区间，对“同数据集不同压缩”对比的增益均接近零；在唯一留出的操纵类型上，公平变体仍低于纯主干。对抗分支按原设无益且削弱其自身条件估计器；在公平配方下未测试。真实CRF多样性较合成JPEG增强带来+7.3点AUC（单次运行，区间不重叠）。

Conclusion: 在FaceForensics++、GAN时代、单编解码器情境下，压缩鲁棒性更多来自“编码多样性数据”而非频域特征或对抗压缩不变性。研究应在比较时匹配模型容量与训练配方；实践上优先投资于多编解码与多质量数据，而非复杂频域/对抗模块。

Abstract: Frequency features and compression-invariant representation learning are widely assumed to be key to deepfake detection that survives video compression. We test this with CAFRL - block-DCT and FFT-phase streams, compression-level-conditioned band attention, and adversarial (gradient-reversal) compression invariance - and report a controlled negative. Under a pre-registered protocol with capacity- and augmentation-matched controls, a plain EfficientNet-B0 on multi-quality data beat CAFRL as specified at every compression level on the FaceForensics++ test split, by 3.66 AUC points at CRF 40 (paired, single seed). A self-audit of our own negative found four defects biased against the frequency hypothesis, and pre-specified re-tests repairing all four showed the deficit to be a recipe artifact, not an architecture failure: the baseline recipe recovered 3.96 points over the matching shipped-recipe variant. The frequency path made no detectable difference: discriminative alone (standalone validation AUC 0.91-0.98 late in training) but of no marginal value under this fusion, at two feature widths of one 4.0 M trunk, every seed-pooled interval for the intra-dataset compression contrasts including zero; on the single held-out manipulation tested, the fair variants sat below the plain backbone. The adversarial branch, as specified, added nothing and degraded its own conditioning estimator; at the fair recipe it is untested. Robustness under single-pass H.264 re-encoding came instead from data diversity: real constant-rate-factor variants beat synthetic JPEG augmentation by 7.3 points (single runs, non-overlapping intervals). The evidence is FaceForensics++-family, GAN-era and single-codec. Match controls on training recipe as well as capacity, and buy compression robustness with codec diversity before architecture.

</details>


### [17] [FLM: Frequency-Aware Language Models for Generative Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28687)
*Jiarun Chen,Kejun Wu,Li Li,Chengtao Cai,Zhengguo Li,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FLM：一种频域感知的语言模型，通过对量化DCT系数进行自回归概率建模以提升图像压缩效率，同时保持确定性重建；在多个数据集上显著优于JPEG和生成式压缩方法，并兼容有损与无损重压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式有损压缩利用先验在低码率下表现出色，但会引入与源内容不一致的纹理与语义偏差，削弱保真度；需要一种既能利用强概率建模提升压缩率、又能避免生成偏差并保持确定性解码的方案。

Method: 将输入图像做块DCT并量化，采用宏块级系数离散化为token序列；用频域语言模型FLM做“下一系数预测”，自回归估计条件分布以供算术编码生成紧凑码流；解码端由LLM与算术解码器联合恢复频域数据，再做逆变换重建；构建频域专用数据集并用两阶段微调以适配多码率；框架兼容有损压缩与JPEG无损重压缩。

Result: 在Kodak、Tecnick、CLIC2020上相对JPEG获得BD-PSNR分别+3.30、+3.83、+3.80 dB；在主观质量上更高的语义一致性并抑制区块伪影；在无损重压缩任务上也具竞争力。

Conclusion: FLM通过频域自回归概率建模与确定性重建结合，突破了生成式压缩的保真度问题并带来更优的率失真性能，具通用性与实用价值，适用于多码率及JPEG重压缩场景。

Abstract: Generative models have significantly improved the performance ceiling of image lossy compression at low bitrates by exploiting learned priors. However, the generated textures and semantic details may deviate from the source content, thereby affecting the fidelity of image reconstruction. To solve these challenges, we propose FLM, a frequency-aware language model that improves compression efficiency through frequency-domain probabilistic modeling while retaining deterministic reconstruction. At the encoder, the input image is transformed into quantized DCT coefficients, which are organized into discrete sequences using macroblock-based coefficient tokenization. FLM then performs next-coefficient prediction to autoregressively estimate token-wise conditional probability distributions for arithmetic coding, thereby generating a compact bitstream. At the decoder, the LLM and arithmetic decoder jointly recover the frequency-domain data, followed by inverse transformations for image reconstruction. A task-specific frequency-domain dataset and a two-stage fine-tuning strategy are further developed to enable the model to operate across multiple bitrate settings. FLM is a versatile compressor that is compatible with both lossy compression and lossless JPEG recompression frameworks. Experiments show that FLM exceeds conventional and generative lossy compression methods in rate-distortion performance. FLM achieves BD-PSNR gains of 3.30 dB, 3.83 dB, and 3.80 dB than JPEG baseline on Kodak, Tecnick, and CLIC2020, respectively. Better qualitative quality of FLM can be achieved in improving semantically high fidelity and suppressing blocking artifacts. FLM is also validated to be applicable to the lossless recompression task with competitive performance.

</details>


### [18] [Projection-Aware End-to-End Learned Video Compression for 360-Degree Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28689)
*Niloofar Maani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文比较了多种360°视频投影格式在端到端神经压缩中的影响，发现等距长方投影（ERP）及其填充变体在光流型神经编解码下最省码率，而立方体与菱形十二面体等多面投影更适合传统块基混合编解码；投影效率具有编解码器依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 360°视频需先从球面映射到2D投影再压缩；不同投影会影响空间连续性、采样均匀性、运动估计与压缩效率。以往对传统编码器（如HM）已有对比，但对端到端神经压缩的系统性评估不足，尤其缺少与JVET 360Lib一致的多格式比较与可微分管线的对照。

Method: 选取JVET 360Lib支持的7种投影格式，基于JVET测试序列与公共测试条件。流程：源ERP→目标编码投影→多码率神经压缩（基于scale-space flow的光流模型）→重建→逆投影回ERP。指标包含PSNR、S-PSNR、WS-PSNR与BD-Rate。并将包含可微投影转换+神经压缩+逆投影的端到端可微管线与360Lib离线流程对比。

Result: 在光流型神经压缩下，等距长方（ERP）与填充ERP获得最佳码率-失真表现；立方体系（如等角立方体、调整立方体）与菱形十二面体较差。与HM-16.16的结果相反：HM中立方体系（尤其等角与调整立方体）优于ERP。可微端到端管线的趋势与结论与360Lib流程一致。

Conclusion: 投影选择的最优性取决于编解码范式：光流驱动的端到端神经模型受益于单面连续投影（ERP类）的空间连续性，而块基混合编码器更能适配多面布局（立方体类）。为学习式360°视频压缩选择投影应优先考虑单面连续性；对传统编码则可倾向立方体变体。

Abstract: 360-degree video supports immersive applications such as virtual reality, autonomous driving, and education. Because spherical content cannot be processed directly by conventional video codecs, it must first be mapped to a two-dimensional projection. Projection choice affects spatial continuity, sampling uniformity, motion estimation, and compression efficiency.
  This thesis investigates how projection format influences end-to-end neural compression of 360-degree video. Seven formats supported by JVET 360Lib are evaluated using the scale-space flow model, JVET test sequences, and common test conditions. Each sequence is converted from its source equirectangular projection to a coding projection, compressed at multiple rate points, reconstructed, and converted back. Performance is assessed using PSNR, spherical PSNR, weighted spherical PSNR, and Bjøntegaard delta rate. A differentiable pipeline combining projection conversion, neural compression, and inverse projection is also compared with 360Lib.
  Results show that equirectangular and padded equirectangular projections provide the highest compression efficiency with the scale-space flow model, while cubemap-based and rhombic dodecahedron projections are less effective. This differs from the conventional HM-16.16 codec, for which cubemap-based formats, particularly equi-angular and adjusted cubemap projections, outperform equirectangular formats. Neural models based on optical flow benefit from the spatial continuity of single-face projections, whereas block-based hybrid codecs better accommodate multi-face layouts. These findings show that projection efficiency is codec-dependent and provide guidance for selecting projections for learning-based 360-degree video compression.

</details>


### [19] [Defending Wearable VLMs Against Private Attribute Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28691)
*Zhimin Li,Pan Wang,Jingxian Chen,Yuantao Tang,Anthony Chen,Qian Lou,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究可穿戴视觉语言模型在拆分推理中如何在保留辅助效用的同时防止中间视觉token泄露隐私，提出TGAP在离开受信设备前对视觉token进行残差变换以压制隐私属性并保持任务效用，实验显示隐私推断大幅下降而效用基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴VLM需将自我视角视频编码为紧凑视觉token并传给下游语言推理；尽管最终文本回答可能无害，但中间token可能被不可信组件或攻击者反推佩戴者/路人隐私属性（地点、收入、性别、兴趣）。现有粗粒度遮挡/注意力屏蔽会严重损害效用，缺少针对“token接口”的系统性隐私-效用联合方案与评价基准。

Method: 构建隐私-效用对偶基准（3,221条图像-问题对），含一个效用问题与四类隐私标签；提出Token-Guided Attribute Privacy (TGAP)：在LLM前的视觉token层学习一个残差变换，联合优化四项目标——(1) 效用保持；(2) 身份正则；(3) 语义隐私抑制；(4) 图像驱动表示抑制，从而在不采用硬遮挡或注意力掩码的情况下减少隐私可识别性。

Result: 在源模型基准上，隐私识别准确率由56.7%降至7.4%（绝对下降49.3个百分点），同时在“放宽”的效用指标下维持74.4%的性能，相比粗暴屏蔽方法显著更优地兼顾隐私与效用。

Conclusion: 保护紧凑token接口是一条切实可行的路径：通过在受信边界内对视觉token进行可学习的隐私抑制并保持任务相关信息，可在可穿戴多模态AI场景中显著降低隐私泄露而不牺牲过多辅助效用。

Abstract: Wearable VLM pipelines promise continuous multimodal assistance from egocentric visual capture: a user asks a task-driven question about the surrounding scene, and the system uses compact visual tokens to support language reasoning. The challenge motivating this work is that the same egocentric evidence needed for useful assistance can also reveal private attributes about the wearer or nearby bystanders. We investigate this as a joint privacy-utility problem for split VLM inference, where visual encoding occurs within a trusted device boundary but intermediate visual tokens may be transmitted to downstream reasoning components. This exposes an understudied leakage surface: even when final textual responses are benign, external attackers or untrusted downstream components can recover private attributes from transmitted visual tokens. To evaluate this tension, we construct a paired privacy-utility benchmark with 3,221 image-question records, each paired with a utility question and privacy labels covering location, income, sex, and interests. We further propose Token-Guided Attribute Privacy (TGAP), a pre-LLM token disentangler that learns a residual transformation of visual tokens before they leave the trusted boundary. TGAP combines utility preservation, identity regularization, semantic privacy suppression, and image-driven representation suppression, avoiding the utility loss caused by coarse hard or attention masking. On the benchmark used for source-model evaluation, TGAP reduces privacy accuracy from 56.7\% to 7.4\%, a 49.3\% absolute drop, while maintaining relaxed utility at 74.4\%. These results suggest that securing the compact token interface is a practical path toward privacy-preserving wearable multimodal AI.

</details>


### [20] [Growing a Stand, Not a Tree: Joint Canopy Generation Reproduces Crown Shyness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28692)
*Guang Yang,Fengchen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文以“树冠羞避”现象为检验，提出用集合生成的流匹配模型生成整片林分树冠，依靠树与树间注意力捕捉只存在于相互关系中的结构；与逐树生成相比，能显著降低林冠间隙分布误差，并在密度外推与实地数据上保持一致性，证明羞避是林分层级属性而非单树属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树冠羞避的关键结构存在于相邻树之间而非单个体上，传统逐树几何或独立生成难以捕捉。作者借此检验生成模型能否学习“只存在于对象之间”的关系结构，并希望在训练-测试分布变化（干扰密度）与实测对比中验证泛化与可校准性。

Method: 将林冠生成表述为集合生成问题，采用基于流匹配（flow-matching）的生成模型，以跨树注意力作为唯一耦合通道；训练数据来自不可化约为逐树几何的资源竞争生长仿真；与等容量的逐树独立生成模型做对照，并在不同树干密度与实地热带栎林数据上进行标定与评测（标定少量标量：干预间隙尺度、树干抖动）。

Result: 联合模型将间隙净空（clearance）分布误差减半，且在超出训练密度范围的林分仍保持优势；对比实测数据，仅用一个标定标量即可在间隙尺度与树冠不对称上达到留出一致性；间隙方向统计主要由树干空间分布（位置/抖动）决定而非生长规则，一旦校准抖动即可与实测吻合。

Conclusion: 树冠羞避在仿真与学习模型中均体现为林分层级属性而非单树属性；基于注意力的集合流匹配能捕捉并泛化这种跨个体关系结构，在分布外密度与实地数据上表现稳健，可作为生成“关系性结构”的有效范式。

Abstract: In closed forests, neighboring tree crowns often stop short of touching, leaving a network of narrow gaps known as crown shyness. The pattern belongs to the stand rather than to any single tree, which makes it a natural probe of a question in generative modeling: can a learned model produce a set of objects whose defining structure exists only between them? We formulate stand-level canopy generation as set generation with a flow-matching model, in which attention between trees is the only channel through which coupling can arise. Trained on stands grown by a resource-competition simulation that is provably not reducible to per-tree geometry, the joint model halves the clearance distribution error of an identical-capacity model that generates each tree alone, and the advantage persists at stem densities outside the training range. Against field measurements of a tropical oak forest, a single calibrated scalar yields held-out agreement in gap magnitude and crown asymmetry. The directional statistics of the gaps are controlled by stem placement rather than by the growth rule, and match the field once stem jitter is calibrated. Crown shyness, in both the simulation and the learned model, is a property of the stand and not of the tree.

</details>


### [21] [SNF-Bench: Separating Static Drift from Natural Flow in Long-Horizon Fixed-Camera Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28694)
*Matiur Rahman Minar,Seunghun Oh,Ganghyeon Jeong,Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SNF-Bench提出面向固定机位的长时段视频生成评测，将画面分解为静态支撑与动态流两部分，分别报告静态保真、动态流的持续性与幅度，以及漂移泄漏，避免单一全帧指标混淆。通过注入可控的平移/旋转/缩放漂移与后期冻结等扰动进行机制验证。实测表明传统全帧运动指标会把背景漂移误判为“好运动”。SNF-Bench关注运动发生在何处及其是否持续，不评价物理真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时段视频评测多用全帧运动与时序一致性指标。对于固定机位自然景（如水、火、烟、雨），期望的是前景/流体的持续运动，而背景应保持静止。全帧指标将背景漂移与期望运动混为一谈，可能高分却场景漂移，或一致却运动停滞。需要能区分“哪里在动、是否持续”的评测框架。

Method: 提出SNF-Bench：1) 将场景分区为静态支撑区域与动态流区域；2) 分别报告三类指标：静态保真（static fidelity）、动态流持续性与绝对幅度（flow persistence with absolute magnitude）、漂移泄漏（drift leakage）；3) 不汇为单一分数，且将漂移泄漏作为解读上下文而非主指标；4) 机制验证而非主观相关：对真实生成视频注入可控的全局平移、旋转、缩放漂移，以及渐进式后期冻结，要求各指标对目标扰动单调响应且对非目标扰动保持选择性；5) 审核公开的长时段文本条件及图像条件模型，在统一推理配置与参考流水线上对比，并提供部署敏感性面板。

Result: 发现全帧运动与静态区漂移对同一输出给出近乎相反排序；在最大可控平移下，fBD与NBF分别升至1.86×与1.32×基线，而全帧Dynamic Degree仅到1.07×，甚至“奖励”了被注入的漂移腐化；说明传统全帧指标会误把背景漂移当作有益运动。

Conclusion: SNF-Bench能区分静态和动态区域，分别衡量静态保真、动态持续与漂移泄漏，避免全帧指标的混淆，适合固定机位长时段视频评测；它关注运动发生位置与持续性，但不评估物理真实度。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation is evaluated with whole-frame metrics that reward motion and temporal consistency. For fixed-camera nature scenes this creates an ambiguity: motion of water, fire, smoke, or rain is desirable, whereas motion of the background is an error. A system can therefore score well on motion while its scene drifts, or on consistency while its flow stagnates. We introduce SNF-Bench, an evaluation framework for long-horizon fixed-camera generation that partitions each scene into static support and dynamic flow and reports static fidelity, flow persistence with absolute magnitude, and drift leakage separately, never as one score. Drift leakage is interpretive context rather than a headline measurement. Each factor is validated mechanistically rather than by correlation with preference: we inject global translation, rotation, and scale drift and progressive late freezing at known severity into real generations, and require each factor to respond in its stated direction and to remain selective against corruptions it does not target. Auditing publicly released long-horizon text-conditioned checkpoints under one recorded common inference configuration, plus an image-conditioned track with released-pipeline references and a deployment-sensitivity panel, we find that whole-frame motion and static-region drift induce near-opposite orderings of the same outputs. At maximum controlled translation, fBD and NBF rise to $1.86\times$ and $1.32\times$ baseline, but whole-frame Dynamic Degree reaches only $1.07\times$---rewarding the corruption. SNF-Bench measures where motion occurs and whether it persists; it does not measure physical realism. Project page: https://minar09.github.io/snfbench/.

</details>


### [22] [Weaving Visual Narratives: Agentic Image Bundle Composition Beyond Atomic Visual Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28695)
*Rong Shan,Tianyi Xu,Congmin Zheng,Wenteng Chen,Jiachen Zhu,Junjie Wu,Teng Wang,Weiwen Liu,Changwang Zhang,Weinan Zhang,Jun Wang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出从“原子式”单图检索转向“图像捆（bundle）”组合与检索的新范式：按查询动态组合一组结构相关、能叙事的图片，而非单独打分排序。为此构建IBCBench数据集，并提出BundleWeaver框架，用LLM逐步推断缺失关系角色、用VLM做整组验证，在组合空间中高效搜索，显著优于现有嵌入与分解-重排方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人相册检索中，用户常想要一个能讲述结构化小故事的图片集合，而不是若干无关单图。传统点式匹配无法建模“非可分解的联合相关性”，导致对关系结构视而不见（relational blindness）。因此需要从“给每张图打分”转向“按查询组装关系一致的图像捆”。

Method: 1) 定义Image Bundle Composition任务：给定查询，从庞杂无结构图片池中动态组合满足多角色、多关系约束的图片捆。2) 构建IBCBench：含109,467张图、667条经半自动验证的查询。3) 提出BundleWeaver：把IBC表述为“条件化增量超边发现”。具体做法：- 由LLM基于查询与当前部分捆，主动搜索/填补缺失的关系角色与候选；- 由VLM对候选整捆进行整体一致性与相关性验证；- 迭代式组合与裁剪以高效探索组合空间。

Result: 在广泛实验中，SOTA嵌入检索与静态“分解-重排”策略难以捕捉跨图关系，性能显著落后；BundleWeaver取得大幅提升，验证了动态关系组合与整捆评估的有效性。

Conclusion: 检索应从单图原子打分转向面向叙事与结构关系的“图像捆”动态组合。IBCBench为该方向提供基准，BundleWeaver展示了用LLM+VLM进行增量关系发现与整捆验证的有效路径，推动面向联合相关性的检索研究。

Abstract: Image retrieval has traditionally been formulated as a point-wise matching problem, where each candidate image is scored in isolation. However, this atomic paradigm fails to capture the complexity of human search intent within personal photo collections, where users often seek compact visual stories bound by structural relations rather than isolated snapshots. To address this limitation, we introduce **Image Bundle Composition (IBC)**, a novel paradigm that shifts the objective from ranking individual images to dynamically composing cohesive image bundles from a massive, unstructured photo pool. Since target bundles are not predefined, IBC presents a severe combinatorial explosion challenge and demands modeling non-decomposable joint relevance. To establish this paradigm, we construct **IBCBench**, the first IBC benchmark dataset containing 109,467 images and 667 verified queries, built via a semi-automated verification pipeline. Furthermore, we propose **BundleWeaver**, an agentic framework that reformulates IBC as query-conditioned incremental hyperedge discovery. By employing a Large Language Model to adaptively search for missing relational roles and utilizing a Vision-Language Model for whole-bundle verification, BundleWeaver effectively navigates the combinatorial space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that while state-of-the-art embedding models and static decompose-and-rerank paradigms suffer from relational blindness, BundleWeaver achieves substantial performance gains, highlighting the necessity of shifting from atomic scoring to dynamic relational composition. Our dataset and code are available.

</details>


### [23] [Instruction Distillation: Text Instructions as Visual Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28696)
*Hardik Jindal,Soumyabrata Pal,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“指令蒸馏”以替代多模态LLM视觉ICL中高成本的图像示例，将每张训练图像蒸馏为结构化识别指令，保留类内多样性。在推理时用指令或指令+少量图像检索，达到与图像ICL相当或更优的精细分类性能，同时显著降低token与延迟；图像与指令信号互补，混合方案最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉ICL需要检索多张相似样例，但每张图像上下文占用大量token，K大时推理代价高；现有按“类别级”描述会丢失类内差异，限制泛化。需要一种既保留细粒度差异又降低推理成本的上下文形式。

Method: 离线阶段：用MLLM对每张训练图像生成结构化识别指令，包含外观线索、与相似类的区分特征、常见混淆点；同时建立统一CLIP检索索引。在线阶段：比较五种配置——零样本、仅图像ICL、仅指令ICL、以及两种图像-指令混合（将检索邻居在图像与指令间分配）。在7个细粒度基准与2个MLLM骨干上评测。

Result: 仅指令ICL在K=1时可匹配或优于仅图像ICL；在K=5时将每次查询token降低约2.9倍、推理时延降低约3.3倍。混合配置显示图像与文本信号互补，提升上下文质量与最终性能。

Conclusion: 将图像示例蒸馏为每图像的结构化指令，既保留细粒度类内多样性，又显著降低推理成本；在多基准与骨干上验证有效。图像提供视觉模式，指令提供显式规则，二者结合效果最佳。

Abstract: Visual in-context learning (ICL) with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is effective for fine-grained visual classification, but each retrieved image example consumes several hundred context tokens, making large-$K$ settings prohibitively expensive at inference scale. We propose Instruction Distillation: an offline procedure in which the MLLM itself generates, for each individual training image, a structured identification instruction encoding general appearance cues, features that differentiate the class from visually similar ones, and a common confusion point. Unlike prior work that produces a single description per class, our instructions are generated per training image, preserving the intra-class visual diversity that per-class descriptions collapse. At inference time, we study five configurations sharing a single CLIP retrieval index: zero-shot, image ICL, instruction-only ICL, and two hybrid variants in which retrieved neighbors are split between images and instructions. Across seven fine-grained benchmarks and two MLLM backbones, instruction based pipelines match, or exceeds image ICL at $K{=}1$ and reduces per-query tokens by $2.9\times$ and inference latency by $3.3\times$ at $K{=}5$. Hybrid configurations further show that visual and textual ICL signals are complementary, images give visual patterns to learn and see, while instructions give explicit rules and logic. When both of these are provided, the quality of context improves, which is noticeable in the performance.

</details>


### [24] [State-Conditioned Visual Evidence Retrieval for Fine-Grained Perception in Document Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28698)
*Mingxu Chai,Chenyu Liu,Ziyu Shen,Jiazheng Zhang,Kaidi Zhang,Ruoyu Chen,Jun Long,Jihua Kang,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出在自回归解码中进行“按需取证”的细粒度感知：先用全局紧凑表示把握粗结构，再根据当前token状态检索少量高分辨率局部区域，从而兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档解析需要比一般视觉-语言任务更细粒度的视觉信息，但现有VLM多用全局压缩视觉token，细节被混叠在单一表示里，解码每步都重复访问整幅表示，既低效又影响细粒度感知。作者观察到每次预测所需的视觉证据往往是局部且与当前解码状态相关，因此需要一种能按状态检索局部高分信息的机制。

Method: 提出SCVER（State-Conditioned Visual Evidence Retrieval）：解码时仅保留紧凑的全局表示用于粗结构理解，并基于当前token状态从高分辨率图像中检索少量相关区域进行细粒度感知，形成粗到细的计算路径。为解决状态条件检索学习不稳定问题，提出空间引导学习目标（SGLO），利用空间监督/引导信号稳定检索训练。

Result: 在多项文档解析基准上，SCVER在降低输入分辨率条件下更稳健，并在准确率-效率折中上优于现有方法，表明按需检索细粒度证据的有效性。

Conclusion: 将文档解析的感知建模为自回归状态条件下的按需视觉证据检索，可在保持或提升精度的同时显著提高计算效率；引入SGLO可稳定训练这一检索过程。

Abstract: Compared with typical vision-language tasks, document parsing places stronger demands on fine-grained visual perception. Existing vision-language model (VLM)-based parsing approaches rely on globally compressed visual tokens, where fine-grained details are entangled within a single representation and repeatedly accessed during decoding. However, we observe that the visual evidence for each prediction is typically localized and conditioned on the current decoding state, whereas such representations must be accessed in full at every decoding step, resulting in inefficient computation. To address this mismatch, we formulate perception as state-conditioned visual evidence retrieval (SCVER) during autoregressive decoding. The model operates on a compact global representation for coarse structure and retrieves a small set of relevant high-resolution regions conditioned on the current token state. This coarse-to-fine design enables on-demand access to fine-grained visual cues, relieving globally shared representations from encoding all fine-grained details. We further find that learning such state-conditioned retrieval in VLMs is challenging and unstable. To stabilize this process, we introduce a Spatially-Guided Learning Objective (SGLO) to guide the retrieval process. Experiments on document parsing benchmarks show that SCVER improves robustness under reduced input resolution and achieves a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off, demonstrating the effectiveness of on-demand visual evidence retrieval for fine-grained perception.

</details>


### [25] [Beyond Visual Boundaries: Rethinking Scene Segmentation for Movie RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28699)
*Dong-Hee Kim,Seonwoo Choi,Changbeen Kim,Jungmyung Wi,Juyeon Ko,Youngju Choi,Il Hyeon Mun,Hyunwoo J. Kim,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文探讨在长篇电影理解中使用检索增强生成（RAG）的关键：将“场景分段”作为检索单元是否有效，并提出以叙事为中心的新数据集NarraScene，证明叙事驱动的分段优于均匀切片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解难在：稀疏采样丢细节，密集采样超上下文长度；RAG能折中，但效果取决于检索单元质量。电影理解需要跨小时的角色、事件与叙事弧推理，而传统场景分段可能成为理想检索单元。现有基准更偏重视觉转场而非叙事事件，可能限制RAG效用。

Method: 1) 系统评估现有场景分段方法在下游电影理解任务中的RAG检索单元效用，与“均匀时间切片”对比；2) 审核标准分段基准的标注偏差（视觉显著转场优先）；3) 构建NarraScene：以三层认知分类（物理、角色、叙事变化）标注，且仅当存在叙事层级转变时才设为有效边界；4) 用NarraScene生成的叙事片段作为检索单元，测试下游表现。

Result: 现有场景分段方法在下游RAG任务中普遍不优于均匀切片；标准基准注重视觉而忽视叙事结构；使用NarraScene的叙事片段作为检索单元，显著优于均匀切片。

Conclusion: 电影RAG中的核心挑战不在“检测任意边界”，而在“识别对理解有用的叙事情节单元”。以叙事为中心的场景分段能更好支撑长视频理解与生成。

Abstract: Understanding long-form video remains a fundamental challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Sparse frame sampling fails to capture fine-grained visual details, while dense sampling quickly exceeds context length limits. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) offers a promising middle ground by selectively retrieving relevant video segments for grounded generation, yet its effectiveness critically depends on the quality of the video segments used as retrieval units. In this paper, we investigate RAG for movie understanding, which demands story-level reasoning over characters, events, and narrative arcs spanning hours of content. Scene segmentation, a long-studied problem that partitions movies into semantically coherent units, is a natural candidate for defining such retrieval units. We reexamine whether existing methods actually serve this role through comprehensive evaluation on downstream movie understanding tasks, and find that they consistently fail to outperform naive uniform temporal chunking. Our audit of the most standard scene segmentation benchmarks reveals why: current annotations prioritize visually salient transitions over narrative event structure. Motivated by this mismatch, we introduce NarraScene, a narrative-centric scene segmentation dataset annotated with a three-level cognitive taxonomy spanning physical, character, and narrative change, where every valid boundary requires a narrative-level shift. When used as retrieval units, these narrative-grounded segments outperform uniform chunking on downstream movie understanding tasks, suggesting that the central challenge for scene segmentation in movie RAG is not detecting boundaries, but identifying the narrative event units that matter for movie understanding.

</details>


### [26] [TopoAgent: A Structure-Aware Perception-to-Reasoning Framework for Diagram-to-Graph Topology Extraction with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28701)
*Bangwei Guo,Xujiang Zhao,Yanchi Liu,Wei Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Dongyue Li,Masaharu Morimoto,Takayuki Kuroda,Dimitris Metaxas,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TopoBench-180基准与TopoAgent框架，面向从结构化图示中提取图拓扑；在180张图示上验证，显著提升特别是边的抽取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在图示到图的转换中，缺乏细粒度感知落地与全局一致的拓扑推理能力，导致边/节点关系提取不稳。需要一个标准化基准与一个结构感知的感知-推理一体化方法来推动研究。

Method: 1) 构建TopoBench-180：涵盖Web风格与Network风格的180个结构图，并提供规范化图注释。2) 设计TopoAgent：分阶段流程——(a) 具身化/落地的目标检测与实体对齐；(b) 融合全局结构先验；(c) 规范节点清单构建；(d) 以节点为中心由局部到全局的关系推理；(e) 拓扑一致性约束与纠错，最终逐步生成目标图。

Result: 在TopoBench-180上相对强基线与近期视觉推理框架取得领先，尤其在边的抽取指标上表现显著更好，展示了更可靠的拓扑恢复能力。

Conclusion: 工作补齐多模态结构化理解领域的空白：提供人审的基准与一个结构感知的TopoAgent框架，可促进图示到图拓扑抽取研究与应用；数据与资源将公开发布。

Abstract: Diagram-to-graph topology extraction aims to extract a graph of entities and their connections from a structural diagram. This task remains challenging for current vision-language models because it requires both fine-grained perceptual grounding and topology-aware reasoning with global consistency. We present TopoBench-180, a human-verified benchmark for diagram-to-graph topology extraction, and TopoAgent, a structure-aware perception-to-reasoning framework for reliable topology extraction using large vision-language models. TopoBench-180 contains 180 structural diagrams spanning Web-style and Network-style categories, paired with canonical graph annotations. TopoAgent progressively extracts the target graph by combining grounded perception, global structural priors, canonical node inventory construction, node-centric local-to-global relation reasoning, and topological consistency enforcement. Experiments on TopoBench-180 show that TopoAgent outperforms strong vision-language model baselines and recent visual reasoning frameworks, especially on edge extraction. More broadly, this work fills an important gap in multimodal structured understanding by establishing a benchmark and framework for diagram-to-graph topology extraction. The benchmark and associated resources will be publicly released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/WayneGuo0011/TopoBench-180.

</details>


### [27] [Stochastic Liquid Deformation Fields: An SDE Generalisation of Closed-Form Continuous-Time Cells for Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28702)
*Mingzhao Li,Arghya Pal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将D-3DGS中的MLP替换为闭式连续时间CfC单元，使形变场在推理时具备连续时间特性；进一步在CfC时间门加入高斯噪声，形成无需求解器的训练期SDE版本。合成数据上，随机场≈确定性CfC并多数优于MLP；真实数据上，确定性CfC最佳，加噪无益。工作提供了将CfC解释为SDE的清晰框架，并如实报告噪声何时有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CfC为液体神经网络的闭式解，具有连续时间与高效前向优势，但其常用确定性形式忽略了原始液体网络中被认为提升鲁棒性的随机项。作者希望：1) 将CfC重释为SDE的确定性极限；2) 系统评估在动态场景重建（D-3DGS形变场）中重新引入噪声是否带来鲁棒性与泛化收益。

Method: - 用CfC堆栈替换D-3DGS形变场的MLP，保留闭式时间门与前向推理效率。
- 在每个CfC单元的时间门加入小幅高斯扰动，仅在训练期启用，无需数值SDE求解；推理时关闭即退化为纯CfC。
- 在合成D-NeRF与真实NeRF-DS数据集上比较三者：MLP、确定性CfC、随机CfC(SDE)。

Result: - D-NeRF（合成）：随机CfC性能与确定性CfC相当，且多数场景优于MLP。
- NeRF-DS（真实）：确定性CfC已是最佳，加噪声不带来提升。
- 训练时加噪不影响推理成本与稳定性（推理等同确定性CfC）。

Conclusion: CfC形变场可被自然地视为SDE的确定性极限；在训练期向时间门注入高斯噪声是一种简洁、零求解器开销的做法。其收益依赖数据分布：合成数据上可与或优于确定性方案并胜过MLP，真实数据上噪声未显著助益。工作提供了何时应引入噪声的实证边界与解释。

Abstract: Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting (D-3DGS) reconstructs dynamic scenes by deforming a canonical set of 3D Gaussians through a deformation field of frame time. Replacing its MLP with a stack of Closed-form Continuous-time (CfC) cells-a Liquid Neural Network that solves the Liquid Timeconstant ODE in closed form-gives the field continuous-time behaviour at feed-forward cost. That closed form, however, is only the deterministic limit of a noise-driven system, and drops the stochastic term usually credited for the robustness of liquid networks. We put it back: a small Gaussian perturbation is added to the time gate of every CfC cell, turning the deterministic field into a simple stochastic (SDE) one. The noise is used only during training, needs no solver, and reduces exactly to the CfC when switched off. On the synthetic D-NeRF scenes the stochastic field is on par with the deterministic CfC and beats the MLP baseline on most scenes; on the real-world NeRF-DS scenes the deterministic limit is already best and adding noise does not help. The study thus gives both a clean way to read the CfC field as an SDE and an honest account of when a plain noise term helps and when it does not.

</details>


### [28] [Variable-Granularity Tokenization for High-Resolution Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28706)
*Khayrul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGTok 是一种训练外的自适应分块（tokenization）方法，在进入ViT编码器前按区域自适应设定patch粒度，用固定的全图token预算在空中小目标检测中提升精度并降低算存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ViT检测器使用固定栅格分块，面对原生分辨率航拍图像时，要么细粒度捕捉极小目标、要么受限于算力与显存，二者难兼顾；需要一种在不改动主干或训练的前提下，既控制token数又保留对小目标敏感度的方法。

Method: 提出训练无关的VGTok分词器：1) 对区域计算多尺度形态学top-hat与周围的可分性分数；2) 以图像内百分位阈值选择细粒度区域，从而在全图层面固定token预算；3) 用结构张量的最小特征值λ_min作为门控，仅在存在二维结构的地方细化，使一维杂波保持粗粒度；4) 保证生成的tokens严格划分整幅图像；作为drop-in在Co-DETR+EVA-02 ViT-L中使用。

Result: 在VisDrone-val上，于40%至100%任意token预算均超越已发表结果：40%预算时AP=44.22（序列丢弃约3/5），满密度AP=48.38，较最强公开方法高6.08。迁移到AI-TOD-v2无需改动，AP=37.27，AP_vt=19.51；作为冻结checkpoint的纯插拔，在78.5% tokens时AP=36.29，仍超越所有已发表方法；在相同条件下，以376.3M参数超过3.0B多专家模型。

Conclusion: 在进入主干前按局部分离度与结构几何自适应分配token预算，可在以小目标为主的航拍检测中，保持或提升精度，同时将编码器计算降至约1/3、显存降至约1/2，且方法无需训练、可即插即用并具强迁移性。

Abstract: ViT detectors fix a uniform token grid before any learned stage. A native-resolution aerial detector must then choose between resolving few-pixel objects and staying inside compute and memory limits. We introduce VGTok, a training-free tokenizer that sets patch granularity per region from pixels, ahead of the encoder. VGTok scores each region by multi-scale morphological top-hat separability from its surround, then thresholds those scores at a per-image percentile, which fixes the token budget. A structure-tensor gate ($λ_{\min}$) refines only where two-dimensional object structure supports it, leaving one-dimensional clutter coarse. The resulting token set is a strict partition of the image. In a Co-DETR detector with an EVA-02 ViT-L encoder, VGTok clears every published VisDrone-val AP and AP$_S$ at every budget from 40\% to 100\% of tokens. At 40\% it records 44.22 AP with three fifths of the sequence discarded before the first transformer block; dense, it reaches 48.38 AP, $6.08$ above the strongest published entry. VGTok transfers to AI-TOD-v2 untouched, same scorer and same rank, and sets a new state of the art at 37.27 AP and 19.51 AP$_{vt}$. As a pure drop-in into a frozen checkpoint it reaches 36.29 AP at 78.5\% of tokens, above every published entry, where our 376.3M-parameter detector clears a 3.0B multi-expert model. We show that a token budget fixed before the backbone, from local separability and structure geometry alone, holds accuracy on the tiny-object regimes that dominate aerial detection, at $3.1\times$ less encoder compute and $1.9\times$ less encoder memory. Code and models are available at \href{https://github.com/khayrulbuet13/vgtok}{\texttt{github.com/khayrulbuet13/vgtok}} and \href{https://huggingface.co/khayrulbuet13/vgtok}{\texttt{huggingface.co/khayrulbuet13/vgtok}}.

</details>


### [29] [MANTLE: A Framework for Adaptive In-Situ Planetary Perception Using a Modular Uplink Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28724)
*Pranav Durai,Gary Doran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MANTLE：基于共享DINOv2骨干、多任务头（地貌分类+岩块分割）的火星地表感知网络，分类准确率92.56%，分割IoU 0.753；采用“模块化上行原则”，仅保留冻结骨干在航天器端，后续在地面训练轻量任务头上行更新，实现持续演进的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星探测需要更安全高效的自主导航与科学勘察。地貌分类提供着陆与科学语境，岩块分割支撑避障与路径规划。现有方法难兼顾多任务、跨域泛化与在轨更新成本，亟需一种可扩展、轻量且可持续升级的感知框架。

Method: 构建MANTLE多任务自适应网络：共享DINOv2高层特征骨干，两个任务特定头分别处理——1) 利用HiRISE轨道影像做大尺度地貌分类；2) 利用MSL车载影像做像素级岩块分割。采用模块化设计：骨干冻结驻留在航天器端，任务头可在地面独立训练后上行部署，无需重训全模型。

Result: 地貌分类在7类火星地形上测试准确率92.56%；岩块分割验证集IoU=0.753，并在未见过的漫游路径与日期（cross-sol）测试集上表现稳健，显示良好泛化。

Conclusion: MANTLE证明了共享骨干+可上行轻量任务头的有效性与实用性：在不更改主干的前提下持续扩展感知能力，降低在轨计算与更新代价。作为起点，地貌分类与岩块分割已达高性能，未来可按需添加更多任务头，使探测器在任务周期内不断学习与进化。

Abstract: Planetary surface exploration missions rely increasingly on autonomous robotic platforms capable of interpreting complex terrain to ensure safe navigation, enable targeted science, and improve operational efficiency, as demonstrated across past Mars missions from Viking through Perseverance. Among the key perception capabilities, landform classification provides contextual information for landing site selection and scientific analysis, while boulder segmentation supports hazard assessment and path planning. This paper presents MANTLE, a multi-task adaptive network for terrain and landform extraction. The model uses a shared DINOv2 backbone for high-level feature extraction with task-specific heads: a classification head for large-scale landform classification, and a segmentation head for pixel-wise boulder localization, each trained on curated datasets built respectively from HiRISE orbital imagery and MSL surface-level imagery. The classification head achieved a test accuracy of 92.56% across seven Martian terrain classes, while the segmentation head achieved a validation IoU of 0.753 and showed strong cross-sol generalization on a held-out test set from previously unseen rover traverses. A key advantage of MANTLE is its modular, extensible design, formalized here as the Modular Uplink Principle: only a shared, frozen backbone needs to remain onboard, while subsequent perception capabilities are trained on Earth as lightweight task-specific heads and uplinked without retraining the full model. This work demonstrates two such high-impact capabilities, terrain classification and boulder segmentation, as an initial realization of a framework built to support many more over a mission's lifetime. With this foundation, future explorers need not arrive on Mars fully formed, but can continue to learn, adapt, and grow more capable with every uplink.

</details>


### [30] [Inter-3D VQA: A Roadside Multimodal Benchmark for 3D Spatiotemporally Grounded Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28762)
*Shaozu Ding,Linan Song,Dajiang Suo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Inter-3D VQA：首个基于路侧多模态（同步LiDAR点云+多视角图像）的交叉口3D时空落地VQA基准，含407K问答；并给出融合几何的MLLM基线Inter-Geo与统一评测Inter-Metrics。结果显示Inter-Geo在空间/时间推理上显著优于仅图像模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQA/MLLM在交通场景的评测多基于车载视角或2D路侧视频，难以衡量真实3D距离、轨迹、道路拓扑与关键安全交互等需要3D时空落地的能力，缺少能反映路侧真实交叉口理解与安全推理的基准。

Method: 构建Inter-3D VQA：从同步LiDAR点云与多视角图像生成覆盖车道级位置、目标关系、运动模式与近失事故交互推理的407K问答。提出Inter-Geo：将物体级与场景级对齐的LiDAR表示注入到MLLM，实现几何感知的多模态推理；提出Inter-Metrics：统一评估文本一致性、数值精度与语义正确性。

Result: Inter-Geo在实验中全面优于仅图像的VLM，尤其在需要3D空间与时间落地推理的任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过路侧3D多模态数据与几何对齐的MLLM，能够更准确评测和提升交通场景中的3D时空推理与安全相关理解；基准与代码已开源，促进该方向研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in visual question answering (VQA) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled natural-language reasoning over traffic scenes. However, existing benchmarks are largely built from ego-vehicle views or 2D roadside videos, limiting their ability to evaluate 3D-grounded reasoning over real-world distances, trajectories, infrastructure topology, and safety-critical interactions. We introduce Inter-3D VQA, a large-scale roadside multimodal benchmark for 3D spatiotemporally grounded VQA at intersections. Built from synchronized point clouds and multi-view images, Inter-3D VQA contains 407K QA pairs covering lane-level positions, object relationships, motion patterns, and near-miss-oriented interaction reasoning. We further propose Inter-Geo, an MLLM baseline that integrates object- and scene-level aligned LiDAR representations, and Inter-Metrics, a unified evaluation framework for textual consistency, numerical accuracy, and semantic correctness. Experiments show that Inter-Geo outperforms image-based VLMs, especially on grounded spatial and temporal reasoning tasks. Our benchmark and codes are available at https://github.com/ASU-Suo-Lab/Inter-3D-VQA .

</details>


### [31] [FairReL: Deepfake Detection using Fairness-Aware Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28777)
*Xiaoman Lu,Jiaqi Li,Shuntian Zheng,Huiping Chen,Yu Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FairReL，通过针对多尺度空间特征与微调残差子空间的定向去偏，减少深度伪造检测在不同人群子组上的不公平，同时保持甚至提升跨数据集检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深伪检测总体准确但在人群子组上误差不均，部分群体的真实脸更易被判为伪造。已有公平化方法多对整体现表征施加约束，无法定位与不公平相关的成分，易压制有用伪造线索且仍残留子空间中的群体结构。

Method: 识别两类与子组敏感相关的表征成分：多尺度空间特征与微调诱发的残差特征。以SVD分解的基础模型骨干分离微调残差子空间，并引入两种互补损失：1) 组条件小波去相关（GCWD），在空间小波子带上抑制子组不平衡结构；2) 子空间局部均值对齐（SLMA），在残差子空间内，分别对真实/伪造类内的各子组均值对齐。

Result: 在FF++、Celeb-DF、DFD、DFDC上，相比最新公平化检测器，跨未见数据集AUC提升3.9%，子组FPR差异降低10.2%。

Conclusion: 定向地在空间多尺度与残差子空间施加群体监督可同时提升公平性与泛化性；FairReL在减少子组误报差距的同时提高跨数据集检测效果。

Abstract: Although recent deepfake detectors achieve high overall accuracy, their errors remain unevenly distributed across demographic subgroups, with real faces from certain groups more often misclassified as fake. Existing fairness-aware detectors typically regularise the entire feature representation, without identifying or controlling the specific components that drive unfair predictions. Such coarse intervention can over-suppress useful forgery cues while leaving demographic structure in component-specific subspaces. To address this, we identify two subgroup-sensitive components: multi-scale spatial features, which encode local facial and forgery patterns, and fine-tuning-induced residual features, which adapt the backbone to the unfair training distribution. We propose FairReL, a fairness-aware representation-learning framework that targets both components with dedicated demographic supervision. FairReL uses an SVD-decomposed foundation-model backbone to isolate the fine-tuning-induced residual representation, and introduces two complementary losses. Group-Conditional Wavelet Decorrelation (GCWD) suppresses subgroup-imbalanced structure across spatial wavelet sub-bands, while Subspace-Localised Mean Alignment (SLMA) aligns subgroup means within each real/fake class in the residual representation. Experiments on FF++, Celeb-DF, DFD and DFDC show that, against the state-of-the-art fairness-aware detector, FairReL improves unseen-dataset AUC by 3.9% while reducing subgroup FPR disparity by 10.2%. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaoman89/FairReL .

</details>


### [32] [ClearText-Video: A Large-Scale Text-Centric Video Dataset Bridging Video Restoration and Scene-Text Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28784)
*Jinlong Li,Jiaming Ding,Dingfu Lu,Malcolm Hsiu,Chuang Ke,Kangning Yang,Bochen Guan,Lan Fu,Jie Cai,Huiming Sun,Zibo Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CTVid基准，系统评测文本中心视频在多种画质下的读写与推理鲁棒性，揭示恢复增强与下游文本理解间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实视频常含模糊、压缩噪声、低分辨等劣质因素，导致MLLM在读取场景文本和进行视频推理时不稳健。缺少可控画质变化、具大规模标注与双语问答的基准来系统研究与衡量这一问题。

Method: 构建ClearText-Video(CTVid)：收集4,639条真实文本富集的第一视角视频，提供高质量、匹配的退化质量与恢复质量版本；标注55万+帧、160万+经人工校验的场景文字、22万+时空问答（中英双语）。据此定义两类任务：文本中心视频恢复与多画质视频问答；评测18种恢复方法与16个SOTA多模态大模型。

Result: 发现视觉增强并不必然提升文字保真或推理性能；运动模糊比低分辨更具破坏性；恢复过程可能改变MLLM依赖的文字证据；仅OCR管线显著落后于端到端多模态推理。

Conclusion: CTVid揭示视频恢复与文本扎根理解之间的脱节，为面向恢复感知与画质鲁棒的文本中心视频系统提供了严格评测基础与研究方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently made strong progress in visual--linguistic understanding. However, their performance on text-centric video reasoning remains highly sensitive to input quality. Real-world user-provided videos often contain motion blur, compression artifacts, noise, and low-resolution text, which impair reliable text reading and downstream reasoning. Whether MLLMs can robustly read and reason about real-world scene text under diverse quality conditions remains a fundamental open question. We introduce ClearText-Video (CTVid), a large-scale, scene-text-aware benchmark for studying text-centric video understanding under controlled quality variation. CTVid contains 4,639 real-world text-rich egocentric videos, 550K+ frames, 1.6M human-verified scene-text annotations, and 220K+ spatial/temporal question--answer pairs in Chinese and English. For each high-quality video, CTVid provides content-matched Degraded-Quality and Restored-Quality variants, supporting two task families: Text-Centric Video Restoration and Multi-Quality VideoQA. We evaluate 18 representative restoration methods and 16 state-of-the-art MLLMs on CTVid. The results show that visual enhancement does not guarantee textual fidelity or downstream reasoning gains: blur is more damaging than low resolution, restored videos can alter the textual evidence used by MLLMs, and OCR-only pipelines remain far below direct multimodal reasoning. CTVid exposes the gap between video restoration and text-grounded understanding, providing a rigorous foundation for restoration-aware, quality-robust text-centric video systems.

</details>


### [33] [Beyond Representation Learning: A Systematic Study of Joint-Embedding Predictive Generation for 3D Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28787)
*Meng Zhou,Wenhao You,Yuxing Chen,Yueying Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Med-D-JEPA 将 D-JEPA 扩展到三维脑 MRI：以3D KL-正则化对抗VAE的连续潜变量为“词元”，结合遮蔽上下文预测、表征对齐、逐词元扩散与迭代采样，实现在BraTS与OASIS上的高保真多样生成，并显著提升下游分类与分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 JEPA 主要聚焦于自监督表征学习，D-JEPA 刚在自然图像中显示生成潜力，但三维医学影像（特别是脑 MRI）尚无系统验证；需要一个能兼顾高保真、多样性与下游可用性的3D医学图像生成框架，以缓解数据稀缺与隐私限制。

Method: 构建 Med-D-JEPA：1）用3D KL-正则化对抗VAE将MRI编码为连续潜在词元；2）在潜在空间中进行遮蔽上下文预测与表征级对齐；3）对每个词元施加扩散噪声建模；4）通过迭代的“下一组词元”采样生成完整体数据。评测包括无条件与类别条件生成（BraTS2019、OASIS-1），下游分类，以及基于BraTS2020的初步全肿瘤分割迁移。

Result: 在保真度与多样性指标上优于或可比多种强基线。用合成数据预训练显著提升分类：BraTS2019 AUC从0.63到0.85，OASIS-1从0.78到0.87；分割方面Dice从0.74到0.80，HD95由13.40降至9.56 mm。

Conclusion: 联合嵌入式预测生成在3D医学成像中可行且有效；Med-D-JEPA 不仅提升生成质量，还能显著促进下游分类与分割，值得在3D医学影像合成与数据高效学习中进一步研究与拓展。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) have primarily been developed for self-supervised representation learning. Denoising JEPA (D-JEPA) recently demonstrated strong generative capabilities on natural images, yet the applicability to 3D medical imaging remains unexplored. Building on the D-JEPA framework, we present Med-D-JEPA, a systematic adaptation and evaluation of joint-embedding predictive generation for 3D brain MRI. Med-D-JEPA operates on continuous latent tokens produced by a 3D KL-regularized adversarial variational autoencoder, and combines masked context prediction, representation-level alignment, per-token diffusion, and iterative next-set-of-token sampling. We evaluate unconditional and class-conditional generation quality on BraTS2019 and OASIS-1 datasets; downstream classification utility; and preliminary whole-tumor segmentation on BraTS2020. Across different generation settings, Med-D-JEPA achieves superior or competitive performance compared to several strong baselines on fidelity and diversity metrics. Compared to training with real samples, Med-D-JEPA-based synthetic pretraining improves classification AUC from 0.63 to 0.85 on BraTS2019 and from 0.78 to 0.87 on OASIS-1. In the segmentation study, pretraining on Med-D-JEPA samples improves Dice from 0.74 to 0.80 and reduces HD95 from 13.40 to 9.56 mm. These findings establish joint-embedding predictive generation as a promising direction for 3D medical image synthesis and encourage further research in this direction.

</details>


### [34] [Blind Stereoscopic Omnidirectional Image Quality Assessment Using Predictive Coding Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28798)
*Wei Zhou,André Kaup*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无参考立体全景图像质量评估指标PCH，结合局部合眼感知、全局预测编码分层建模与回归器，利用视口、显著性与双目融合特征，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOI质量评估困难，原因在于自由视角视口、双目视觉与全景场景带来的复杂HVS机制，现有指标难以同时建模局部注意与全局场景推断，需要一种既符合HVS又能在无参考条件下稳定预测质量的模型。

Method: 以HVS和观看SOI流程为导向：1) 从球面图像采样多个视口，做双目合眼（cyclopean）转换与显著性检测，获得局部注意与质量线索并聚合；2) 基于双目机制将左右视图融合，进行预测编码分层（PCH）建模以表征全局场景内在表征与不一致性；3) 设计视觉质量回归器，将局部与全局感知线索联合映射为最终质量分数。

Result: 在多组数据集上进行大量实验，所提PCH在相关性与一致性指标上均取得与或优于SOTA的方法，表现稳定且提升显著。

Conclusion: HVS启发的分层预测与局部-全局联合建模适用于无参考SOI质量评估，PCH能有效捕捉双目与注意机制，显著提升评估精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Stereoscopic omnidirectional images (SOIs) have provided users with newly immersive quality of experience in virtual reality environments. However, developing efficient and accurate perceptual quality assessment metrics for SOIs remains challenging due to many factors such as freely changeable field of views and binocular vision. In this paper, based on the characteristics of the human visual system (HVS), we propose a Predictive Coding Hierarchy-inspired metric (PCH) for blind/no-reference stereoscopic omnidirectional image quality assessment.
