Conclusion: 通过生成式辅助信号与新颖的歧义度获取函数，方法能在无文本起点下有效发现信息量高的样本，并结合高效标注界面降低VG标注成本、提升数据质量与训练效率。

Abstract: Collecting natural-language referring expressions along with region annotations, such as masks or boxes, is a major bottleneck in visual grounding (VG), as annotators must write descriptions that distinguish target regions from visually similar ones. We tackle this by formulating active learning (AL) for VG under the realistic setting where only raw images are available without accompanying text. Since ground-truth text is unavailable, sample selection must estimate which images contain ambiguous regions that would require discriminative referring expressions. To address this, we generate auxiliary region-text pairs using foundation models, and introduce Referred Region Ambiguity, a new acquisition function that measures whether the model's confidence collapses onto a single region or disperses across multiple candidates. It allows our method to prioritize images with strong cross-region competition, which are more informative due to their visual ambiguity. We also design a referring-expression annotation interface that helps annotators quickly focus on writing discriminative language with a few clicks. Experiments on RIS and REC benchmarks show that our AL framework consistently outperforms several AL baselines, while a user study shows up to 1.6X faster description labeling of ours.

</details>


### [209] [Quantum-Grassmann-Plucker Token Mixing for Deep Learning-Based Post-Disaster Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30633)
*Kooroush Farahkhah,Umut Lagap,Taha Rezaei,Saman Ghaffarian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出在卫星图像建筑损伤分类中引入Grassmann‑Plücker（GP）token mixing，并扩展出QGP与HQML‑GP两个分类头；在xBD龙卷风数据上，QGP在已见与未见事件测试集均取得最佳准确率与macro‑F1，表明GP类方法是无需注意力的竞争性替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后建筑损伤评估需要快速准确，但面临类别不平衡、模糊的中间损伤等级以及跨事件泛化差的问题；现有Transformer的token mixing依赖注意力机制，可能在小样本与迁移场景中不稳定，促使作者探索几何代数视角的token表示以提升鲁棒性与泛化。

Method: 将ViT基编码器冻结，输入为配对的灾前/灾后六通道补丁（16×16）；提出三种基于Grassmann‑Plücker的head：GP用Plücker坐标编码token对形成的子空间以表征多尺度关系；QGP在Plücker坐标上加入由幅度构造的概率特征；HQML‑GP将模拟量子电路产生的期望值融入几何表示。与MLP与Transformer头在相同训练与评估协议下比较，并在xBD的Joplin/Moore作开发与已见测试，Tuscaloosa作未见测试。

Result: QGP在测试集上领先：已见事件准确率83.46%、macro‑F1 64.50；未见事件准确率66.45%、macro‑F1 52.70。HQML‑GP验证集macro‑F1最高（65.63%）但测试未超越QGP且训练时间更长。表明GP类token mixing在该任务中具有竞争力。

Conclusion: GP token mixing为成对卫星图像损伤分类提供了注意力之外的有效途径；QGP在准确率与macro‑F1上综合最优且计算性价比更高；HQML‑GP虽有验证优势但泛化与效率不及QGP，显示量子特征需进一步优化以提升跨事件表现。

Abstract: Timely post-disaster building damage assessment from satellite imagery is a critical engineering decision support task, yet it remains constrained by class imbalance, ambiguous intermediate damage states, and limited cross-event transferability. This study presents, to our knowledge, the first application of Grassmann-Plucker (GP) token mixing to computer vision and introduces two extensions for image classification: the Quantum-inspired Grassmann-Plucker (QGP) head and the Hybrid Quantum Machine Learning Grassmann-Plucker (HQML-GP) head. The GP head represents multiscale relationships among image patch tokens by encoding subspaces formed by token pairs with Plucker coordinates; QGP enriches these coordinates with amplitude-derived probability features, whereas HQML-GP incorporates expectation values generated by a simulated quantum circuit into the geometric token representation. Paired pre- and post-event image patches from the xBD tornado dataset were processed using a frozen six-channel Vision Transformer base encoder with 16 x 16-pixel patches. The three GP-based heads were compared with multilayer perceptron and Transformer baselines under identical training, checkpoint selection, and evaluation protocols. Joplin and Moore tornado samples were used for model development and seen-event testing, while Tuscaloosa was reserved for unseen-event evaluation. QGP led both test sets in accuracy and macro-F1: 83.46% and 64.50% for the seen events, and 66.45% and 52.70% for the unseen event. Although HQML-GP obtained the highest validation macro-F1 of 65.63%, it did not surpass QGP on either test set and required substantially more training time per epoch. These results establish GP token mixing as a competitive attention-free alternative to conventional Transformer-based token mixing for paired satellite image damage classification.

</details>


### [210] [Fine-Grained Multi Image Object Hallucination Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30653)
*Joonki Min,Chaeyun Kim,Hyungwook Choi,Yejin Kim,Kihyun Kim,Yohan Jo,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIOH基准，用于系统评测多图场景中的物体幻觉，覆盖四类任务、三种多图推理模式与三类可控对抗压力；实测29个模型揭示即便SOTA也在整合阶段易出错，MIOH为诊断与提升多模态可靠性提供工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态评测多聚焦单图或粗粒度多图，无法细致诊断在多图复杂情境下何种因素诱发物体幻觉；需要一个可控、细粒度、可分解的基准来拆解视觉复杂度与推理需求对幻觉的影响。

Method: 构建MIOH基准：围绕四个基础任务（存在、计数、属性、位置），设计三类多图推理模式（综合、比较、选择），并在三种可控对抗压力（视觉上下文规模、感知难度、上下文偏置）下生成数据与评测协议；对29个MLLM进行系统评测与误差分解。

Result: 29个模型在不同任务与推理模式下表现出显著且可分离的失败模式；即便如GPT-5、Gemini-2.5-Pro等SOTA也会在多图整合环节出现高频幻觉；数据表明幻觉不仅源于感知识别不足，更源自跨图对象表征的维持与整合受限。

Conclusion: MIOH为多图物体幻觉提供可控、细粒度的评测框架，可作为诊断与对比工具，推动MLLM在跨图整合与稳健表征方面的改进，提升多模态系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-image scenarios requiring complex reasoning across visual contexts. However, current MLLMs remain fundamentally limited by object hallucination-generating plausible yet factually inconsistent descriptions about objects. Existing benchmarks, designed primarily for single-image settings or providing only high-level multi-image assessments, cannot systematically diagnose how visual complexity and reasoning demands trigger hallucination. To address this gap, we introduce MIOH, a fine-grained multi-image object hallucination benchmark that systematically evaluates object hallucination across four foundational tasks (existence, counting, attribute, position) through three multi-image reasoning patterns (comprehensive, comparative, selective) under three controlled adversarial pressures (visual context scale, perceptual difficulty, contextual bias). Through evaluation of 29 models, we reveal that even state-of-the-art systems like GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5-Pro exhibit distinct failure patterns across different reasoning patterns and tasks. Our evaluation reveals that hallucination stems not merely from perceptual failures but from integration-stage limitations when maintaining object representations across multiple images. MIOH provides a controlled framework for analyzing multi-image object hallucination and serves as a critical evaluation tool for developing more reliable multimodal AI systems.

</details>


### [211] [APT: Anchor-aligned Perturbations for Tamper Localization in Fully Regenerated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30656)
*Suhyeon Ha,Woo Jae Kim,Joonsung Jeon,Sooel Son,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出APT：一种在扩散重绘（完全再生FR）场景下仍能进行像素级篡改定位的半脆弱潜空间扰动方法，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动篡改定位多假设拼接（SP）场景，背景信号不被破坏；而真实的扩散式修补会对整图去噪重生（FR），使嵌入信号被破坏，现有框架失效。需要一种在FR场景仍可定位篡改的可泛化方法。

Method: 在分发前对图像潜空间特征进行致密、向量级的对齐扰动：令每个空间特征向量朝固定锚定方向对齐。通过“硬负挖掘”损失与“噪声扰动分支”强化全局、均匀的对齐一致性。篡改后，通过比较前景与（锚向对齐的）背景特征的对齐差异来定位篡改区域。

Result: 在COCO上，APT在FR设定的IoU达到0.92，优于最强基线WAM的0.84；而其他现有方法在FR设定几近随机（AUC≈0.5）。

Conclusion: APT在扩散重绘（FR）下实现稳健、细粒度的篡改定位，并能泛化到测试时未知的篡改类型，是实用的取证框架。

Abstract: Proactive tamper localization embeds an imperceptible signal into an image prior to distribution, enabling pixel-level manipulation detection. Existing methods assume a spliced (SP) setting, where synthesized regions are composited onto the original background, leaving embedded signals intact. However, real-world diffusion-based inpainting operates in a fully regenerated (FR) setting, where the entire image undergoes denoising, disrupting background signals and rendering existing frameworks ineffective. We propose APT, a semi-fragile latent-space perturbation that embeds a dense, vector-wise localization signal. By aligning each spatial feature vector toward a fixed anchor direction, APT localizes tampering via the alignment disparity between synthesized foreground and anchor-aligned background features after inpainting. The proposed hard negative mining loss and noisy perturbation branch further enforce uniform alignment. Experiments on COCO demonstrate that APT achieves an FR IoU of 0.92, outperforming the strongest baseline (WAM, 0.84), while existing methods collapse to near-random performance (AUC 0.5), establishing APT as a practical forensic framework generalizable across tampering types unknown at test time.

</details>


### [212] [InfraOcc: An Infrastructure Occupancy Benchmark with Static-to-Dynamic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30657)
*Lei Yang,Xiaokai Bai,Boqi Li,Chunmian Lin,Li Wang,Ziying Song,Jiahuan Zhang,Enhui Ma,Haibao Yu,Jiaqi Ma,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并发布首个面向道路侧（固定视角）三维语义占用基准InfraOcc，并提出面向该场景结构特性的占用推理模型ProSD-Occ；核心发现是静态基础设施占绝大多数且跨帧持久，动态体素极稀疏短暂；方法以“先静后动”的渐进证据推理显著提升动态与总体mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占用估计基准与方法多针对移动载体（自车视角），将占用当作一次性体素分类，未刻画或利用固定视角下“静态持久骨架+稀疏短暂动态”的结构差异。为填补数据与方法空白，建立适配道路侧感知的评价标准与相应推理范式。

Method: 数据方面：构建InfraOcc——固定路侧坐标系、多模态（相机/激光/融合）、290段序列、稠密体素标注；采用静-动解耦的标注流程；统一评测并提供静态/动态占用诊断。模型方面：提出ProSD-Occ（Progressive Static-to-Dynamic Occupancy）：先建模并解释跨帧持久的静态布局，基于静态置信度引导暴露剩余动态证据，再将静态、动态与自由空间证据重组为统一占用场，实现从静到动的渐进式证据推理。

Result: 实证揭示结构性不对称：静态基础设施占97.3%的被占体素且跨帧持久；动态参与者在各位置的中位占用帧占比仅1.8%。在InfraOcc上，ProSD-Occ在所有赛道（相机/激光/多模态）中总体、动态、静态与几何占用指标均排名第一；如相机-only动态mIoU相对最强基线提升23.5%，多模态总体mIoU达65.87。

Conclusion: 固定视角道路侧占用是区别于自车视角的独立问题，应采用“静态优先、动态递进”的证据推理范式。InfraOcc为该方向提供标准化数据与评测，ProSD-Occ验证了利用静-动结构可显著提升动态与整体占用估计表现。

Abstract: Fixed-viewpoint infrastructure sensors repeatedly observe the same traffic space, making roadside 3D occupancy structurally different from ego-vehicle perception: a near-persistent static scaffold is overlaid with sparse, short-lived dynamic events. Existing occupancy benchmarks and methods, however, are built around moving ego vehicles and neither measure nor exploit this structure, instead treating occupancy as flat one-shot voxel classification. We address this gap from both data and model perspectives. We build InfraOcc, to our knowledge, the first real-world infrastructure-side semantic occupancy benchmark, with dense voxel annotations for 290 multi-modal sequences in a fixed roadside frame, a static-dynamic decoupled annotation pipeline, unified camera-only, LiDAR-only, and multi-modal evaluation, and diagnostics for static and dynamic occupancy. InfraOcc shows that static infrastructure fills 97.3% of occupied voxels and persists across frames, whereas dynamic participants have a median occupied-frame ratio of only 1.8% per location, revealing a structural static-dynamic asymmetry beyond semantic long-tailedness. We further propose ProSD-Occ, which reformulates occupancy as progressive static-to-dynamic evidence reasoning: it explains persistent layout, exposes residual dynamic evidence under static-confidence guidance, and recomposes static, dynamic, and free-space evidence into a unified field. ProSD-Occ ranks first in overall, dynamic, static, and geometric occupancy on every track, e.g., a 23.5% relative camera-only dynamic-mIoU gain over the strongest baseline and 65.87 multi-modal overall mIoU, establishing fixed-viewpoint roadside occupancy as a distinct problem with its own reasoning paradigm. The benchmark and code will be publicly available at https://github.com/yanglei18/InfraOcc

</details>


### [213] [UFPR-PEs: A Brazilian Face Recognition Benchmark with Self-Declared Race/Color Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30688)
*Alexandre Diano,Bernardo Biesseck,Gabriel Polo,Vinicius Gregorio,Laura Lopes,Diego Addan,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UFPR-PEs：用巴西政客公共视频构建、带官方自报种族/肤色标注（含巴西特有“parda”）的人脸识别偏差评测基准；在验证与识别（闭集/开集）任务上评测，并按种族与样本难度分析，发现性能强受图像质量影响，群体差距需与难度联动解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸识别广泛部署但在非受控条件下的鲁棒性与跨人群公平性不足；现有基准多基于美欧分类体系，缺失巴西“parda”等细分类，且常使用高质量受控图像，不足以反映真实公共视频环境中的难度与压缩失真。

Method: 构建UFPR-PEs数据集：从公开的巴西当选政客视频中抓取人脸轨迹，基于官方自报的种族/肤色类别进行标注，采用巴西人口普查分类（含parda）；保留压缩与低质、困难样本以贴近真实应用；给出数据构建流程与统计；在验证、闭集识别、开集识别三种设置上进行评测，并进行按种族/难度的子群分析。

Result: 实验显示：人脸识别性能随图像质量显著波动；不同种族/肤色子群的性能差距存在，但这些差距与样本视觉难度高度相关，单独看群体差距可能误导。

Conclusion: UFPR-PEs为研究公共视频场景下的人脸识别偏差提供了可复现实、具人口统计学根基的基准；公平性评估应将视觉难度纳入联合分析，而非仅看群体平均差异。

Abstract: While face recognition systems are widely deployed, ensuring their demographic reliability and robustness under uncontrolled visual conditions remains a critical challenge. To bridge this gap, we present UFPR-PEs, a benchmark for face recognition bias evaluation using public videos of elected Brazilian politicians annotated with official self-declared race/color categories. The dataset adopts the Brazilian census taxonomy, including the parda category, which has no direct equivalent in the U.S.- or Europe-centric schemas commonly used in prior benchmarks. Our benchmark is built from compressed public video and preserves difficult samples so that performance can be analyzed under realistic conditions. We describe the construction pipeline, report dataset statistics, and evaluate face recognition performance across verification and (closed- and open-set) identification settings, including subgroup analysis by race/color and difficulty level. The results show that recognition performance varies substantially with image quality, and that subgroup gaps must be interpreted jointly with visual difficulty rather than in isolation. Overall, UFPR-PEs provides a reproducible and demographically grounded setting for studying face recognition bias under challenging public video conditions.

</details>


### [214] [CANVAS: Consistency-Aware Navigation via Visual Adaptive Sampling for Long-Context Text-to-SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30689)
*Yichen Wu,Haoxuan Qu,Yihang Lou,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CANVAS，一个无需训练的、基于渲染反馈的自回归SVG生成推理框架，通过一致性感知的采样与导航，显著提升全局一致性（几何、布局、遮挡、构图）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归文本到SVG模型虽已能生成复杂矢量图，但标准逐步解码常出现全局不一致（几何关系、空间布局、遮挡顺序、整体构图）的问题，影响可用性与质量。

Method: 训练时不改动模型，推理期引入CANVAS：1）视觉自适应采样，渲染候选未来（rollouts）获得视觉反馈；2）“power-sharpened”轨迹似然重加权以凸显高质候选；3）基于笔画（stroke）级导航规则，估计每个候选笔画在有限生成与渲染预算下的未来价值；4）依据候选的不确定性、决策影响力与展开成本自适应分配采样。

Result: 在多种自回归SVG骨干与多种互补基准上，无需额外训练即提升全局一致性，具体体现在更合理的几何关系、空间布局、遮挡顺序与整体构图。

Conclusion: CANVAS作为一种无需训练、渲染感知的推理框架，能广泛泛化到不同SVG自回归模型与数据集，有效增强文本到SVG生成的全局一致性。

Abstract: Autoregressive large models have recently advanced Text-to-SVG generation from simple icons to complex, long-context graphics, yet standard autoregressive decoding often fails to maintain global consistency across geometry, layout, occlusion, and composition. We introduce CANVAS (Consistency-Aware Navigation via Visual Adaptive Sampling), a training-free, render-aware inference framework that combines power-sharpened trajectory likelihood with visual feedback from rendered futures and derives a stroke-wise navigation rule. It effectively estimates each candidate stroke's future value under a limited generation and rendering budget and adaptively allocates samples according to candidate uncertainty, decision influence, and rollout cost. Experiments across multiple autoregressive SVG backbones and complementary benchmarks demonstrate improvements in global consistency, which includes sound geometric relationships, spatial layouts, occlusion ordering, and overall composition, without additional training, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalization ability of our framework.

</details>


### [215] [Failure or Drift? Evaluating Monocular SLAM under Synthetic and Real-World Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30690)
*Abhay Skaria Thomas,Shashank Agnihotri,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 作者研究在受控图像劣化下，单目SLAM在“跟踪失败”和“持续漂移”两类鲁棒性问题上的差异，并验证合成腐蚀是否能复现真实恶劣环境中的系统排序。结论：学习型跟踪器较少崩溃但常产生严重持续漂移；高物理保真度的雨/雾代理能保持真实排序，简单光照代理不行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中SLAM常因恶劣天气、照明、模糊、传感器瑕疵而失败，但学术评测多在干净序列上。合成腐蚀便于可控评测，但只有当其工程结论与真实条件一致时才有价值。作者想回答：针对单目SLAM，哪些合成腐蚀能忠实反映真实逆境下的系统表现与排序？

Method: 选取一个经典特征点法SLAM与两个学习型跟踪器，在三类劣化上测试：图像空间腐蚀（如亮度变化）、几何感知腐蚀（考虑场景/成像几何的雨雾等结构化扰动）、以及复合腐蚀；并与4Seasons数据集中真实恶劣条件结果对比。评估不只看单一轨迹误差，而是区分显式跟踪失败与在持续运行中积累的漂移。

Result: 学习型跟踪器相较于传统方法更少出现“灾难性丢跟”，但会出现持续且有时很严重的漂移。系统间的相对排序取决于腐蚀的物理保真度：结构化的雨/雾代理能复现真实世界的排序，而简单的光照代理不能，导致对优劣判断出现偏差。

Conclusion: 用于鲁棒性评测的合成腐蚀需具备足够物理逼真度，否则会误导工程结论。对单目SLAM，关注区分“失败”与“漂移”两类鲁棒性问题尤为关键；评测应纳入结构化、几何一致的雨/雾等代理，而避免仅用简单光照变换作为鲁棒性代表。

Abstract: Visual SLAM is commonly evaluated on clean trajectories, although deployment failures are often caused by adverse weather, illumination, blur, and sensor artifacts. Controlled corruptions are attractive because they isolate such factors, but a synthetic stress test is useful only when it leads to the same engineering conclusion as the condition it is intended to approximate. This work examines that question for monocular SLAM. We evaluate a classical feature-based system and two learned trackers under image-space, geometry-aware, and compound corruptions, and compare their behavior with adverse conditions from 4Seasons. Rather than reducing robustness to a single trajectory error, the evaluation separates explicit tracking failure from drift accumulated by methods that remain active. The results show that learned trackers largely replace catastrophic loss with sustained, and sometimes severe, drift. More importantly, the apparent ordering of the learned systems changes with the physical fidelity of the corruption: structured rain and fog proxies preserve the real-world ordering, whereas a simple illumination proxy does not. Code is available at: https://github.com/abhaythomas/master_thesis_vslamlab_robustness.

</details>


### [216] [Can Video World Models Track Unobserved World States?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30692)
*Joonghyuk Shin,Yicong Hong,Jaesik Park,Xun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出高保真视频生成并不等于正确维护“隐藏状态”。在一个动作条件的贝壳游戏基准上，常见视频世界模型在训练范围外迅速退化为随机，而两类可携带并原地更新状态的机制可外推成功：允许负特征值的线性注意力与带非线性快权的TTT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型常被当作模拟器，但像素级逼真不足以证明模型跟踪了不可见的世界状态。为诊断这一差距，作者设计了将可视化渲染与底层隐藏状态组合解耦的“贝壳游戏”基准，聚焦于跨时间的状态保持与外推能力。

Method: 提出动作条件视频Shell Game（视觉版S5跟踪），训练期含5次交换（swap），测试时延长交换链检验外推。比较多类架构：双向与自回归Transformer、Mamba、线性注意力（限制为非负过渡特征值）、以及允许负特征值的线性注意力；并评估TTT（test-time training）带非线性快权。通过扩散像素目标训练与附加去噪步数分析是否帮助。

Result: 受像素扩散目标限制，模型从未直接监督到不可见状态；因此帧本身不携带隐藏状态，需由架构内部状态承载。Transformer的追加式KV缓存导致需在每个chunk从历史重新推断，致使在>5次交换时性能跌至随机，尽管生成视频仍逼真，增加去噪步无益。Mamba与受限线性注意力同样失效。两种能外推的机制：1）线性注意力在允许负过渡特征值后成功；2）TTT以非线性快权更新自身读写特征图，能跨chunk携带并就地修正状态。

Conclusion: 视觉保真不等于状态忠实。要实现可外推的世界建模，体系结构需显式携带并原地更新隐藏状态，而非依赖像素监督或仅追加式记忆。允许符号多样的过渡动力学（如线性注意力的负特征值）或引入可在线修正的快权/TTT机制，是建设“有状态”的视频世界模型的关键方向，并在更复杂的动态探索任务中同样重要。

Abstract: Video world models are increasingly used as simulators, yet visual fidelity alone does not show that a model maintains the hidden state of the world. We examine this gap with an action-conditioned video Shell Game, a visual analog of $S_5$ state tracking that decouples visual rendering from compositing the hidden state underneath. Bidirectional and autoregressive Transformers, Mamba, and linear attention restricted to nonnegative transition eigenvalues all fit the training horizon of 5 swaps and then fall toward chance on longer swap chains (extrapolation) while still rendering plausible video with additional denoising steps providing no benefit. The pixel-based diffusion target never supervises the unseen hidden state, so the generated frames cannot carry it and the state has to live inside the architecture rather than in the tokens. For a Transformer, that architectural state is only an append-only KV cache, so the model has to re-derive the hidden arrangement from the whole history at every chunk. We find two mechanisms that do extrapolate, and both carry a state across chunks and revise it in place. Linear attention succeeds once its transition eigenvalues may be negative, and TTT with a nonlinear fast weight succeeds by updating the feature map through which it reads its own state. We further examine harder cases in dynamic world exploration tasks, and discuss the broader implications for building stateful video world models.

</details>


### [217] [TUE-Detector: A Tool-Using Expert MLLM-Based Detector for AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30704)
*Yichen Wu,Haoxuan Qu,Yongxing Dai,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou,Yuqi Lin,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TUE-Detector：把通用多模态大模型训练成会“用工具找证据并推理”的AI生成视频检测器，能自动调用合适工具挖掘微弱非自然伪迹，实验效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频检测难在于伪迹细微且多样、跨模型/数据分布泛化差。需要一种既能系统化发现可量化“非自然”证据、又能基于证据稳健推理的通用方法。

Method: 提出Tool-Using Expert MLLM框架：将通用MLLM微调为“工具使用专家”，学习在检测流程中（1）选择/调用合适的外部工具（如时空一致性、物理/生理先验、压缩与频域分析等）；（2）收集可度量的非自然证据；（3）对证据进行链式推理给出可靠判别。并进一步设计与构建高质量、任务契合的工具集合与接口以提升效果。

Result: 在多项基准与不同生成器/数据设置上进行大量实验，TUE-Detector在准确率/鲁棒性上优于现有方法，显示出对细微伪迹与跨分布的良好检测能力。

Conclusion: 通过“工具中介的证据发现+MLLM推理”的范式，可将通用MLLM转化为高可靠的AI生成视频检测器；高质量、合适的工具设计是关键，实验验证了该框架的有效性与泛化潜力。

Abstract: AI-generated video detection, which aims to distinguish AI-generated videos from real ones, has recently received increasing research attention. To perform this task reliably, a key challenge lies in accurately identifying subtle-yet-measurable unnatural artifacts. In this work, we address this challenge from a novel perspective of tool-mediated evidence discovery and propose Tool-Using Expert MLLM-based AI-generated Video Detector (TUE-Detector), a novel framework for AI-generated video detection. TUE-Detector trains a general MLLM into a task-tailored tool-using expert detector that learns to invoke suitable tools, collect concrete evidence of unnaturalness, and reason over the evidence for reliable detection. Meanwhile, TUE-Detector further introduces novel designs to equip the expert detector with high-quality and suitable tools. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [218] [VisLens: Single-Pass Interpretable Visual Search for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30705)
*Jingyi He,Sanghwan Kim,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisLens：用logit lens与tuned-lens在单次前向中完成细粒度视觉检索与回答，匹配/超越基线且显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在高分辨率图像中定位小/稀有目标（细粒度视觉搜索）表现欠佳。现有方案：训练免多次查询法虽准确但极慢；RL工具调用法推理快但不透明且难控。需要一种既快速、可解释又准确的方法。

Method: 基于logit lens读取LLM隐藏状态语义，并引入轻量tuned-lens将早期层隐藏表示映射到最终隐藏空间，从早层可读出视觉token；将这些视觉token与查询中的目标词对齐，生成相关区域的裁剪；把裁剪与原图一起一次性送入模型，单次前向完成解码到作答的全流程，无需重复查询或RL策略。

Result: 在细粒度视觉搜索基准上，性能匹配或优于先前方法；推理延迟显著降低：比Thyme快8.5–9.9倍，相比训练免多次搜索法最高快22.2倍。

Conclusion: VisLens实现了单次前向、可解释且高效的视觉搜索，兼具速度与精度，克服了训练免多次与RL工具调用方法的各自缺陷。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) struggle with fine-grained Visual Search, the task of locating small or rare objects in high-resolution images. Existing remedies fall into two families: (1) Training-free methods based on attention or confidence scores are accurate but slow, since they require multiple MLLM queries per example. (2) Reinforcement Learning (RL) trained tool-use models are faster at inference but opaque, since their tool calls remain uncontrollable and hard to interpret. To overcome this, we propose \emph{VisLens} (Visual Focus via Logit Lens), a Visual Search method built on the logit lens, which decodes the semantics held in a hidden state by projecting it through the LLM head. VisLens further uses a lightweight tuned-lens that maps early hidden states into the final hidden state space, so visual tokens can be read out from early layers. These tokens are matched to target words in the query to generate a crop of the relevant region, which is fed back in alongside the original image to produce the final answer. The whole process, from decoding to the final answer, completes in a single forward pass without repeated queries. VisLens matches or exceeds prior baselines while delivering a substantial latency advantage, running $8.5$--$9.9\times$ faster than Thyme and up to $22.2\times$ faster than training-free multi-pass search methods.

</details>


### [219] [RailSyn: Diagnosis-Guided Image Generation for Traceable Data Completion in Railway Foreign Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30709)
*Quan Hao,Chenxi Zhang,Ziyang Tao,Yuyuan Zhou,Yudong Wang,Rui Shi,Lechuan Xu,Changhao Liu,Liguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RailSyn，一个以诊断为导向的合成数据框架，通过“检查器+生成器”闭环，针对铁路异物检测中真实样本稀缺与变化不足问题，定量定位真实数据缺口并有针对性生成补全数据，在九种主流检测器上带来最高+4.9 AP50–95增益，并用C_gap=13.64%量化生成数据对真实缺口区域的覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路异物检测关系行车安全，但真实正样本极少且难以覆盖尺度、入侵关系、场景、光照与恶劣天气等关键变化。现有合成增强虽有效，但缺乏对“补了哪些任务相关缺口”的可解释量化与针对性指导。需要一个能从真实数据出发诊断不足、并驱动生成过程定向补足的框架。

Method: 提出RailSyn，含两部分：1) Inspector（检查器）：基于有限真实样本在表示空间构建可变半径的经验覆盖（variable-radius empirical cover），定位候选“补全区域”，并对合成数据池进行画像与审计，提炼三类需求：铁路上下文、入侵语义、视觉一致性。2) Generator（生成器）：围绕上述需求实施定向生成与优化，包括：域自适应；基于智能体的物体布置与物理接触/入侵关系建模；与生成计划一致的条件式细化。借助Inspector，追踪不同生成变体对表示空间占据的变化，并度量生成数据对真实缺口的覆盖（局部壳层占据C_gap）。

Result: 完整系统达到C_gap=13.64%，表明对由真实数据导出的补全区域有显著覆盖。在九种主流检测器上进行大规模实验，AP50–95最高提升4.9点，且跨架构一致增益，显示方法的普适性与稳健性。

Conclusion: 诊断驱动的“检查-生成”闭环能从真实数据出发发现并量化任务相关缺口，并通过定向合成有效补足，使RFOD在多种检测器上稳定提升；C_gap提供了与任务相关的覆盖度量，可用于指导与评估合成数据质量。

Abstract: Railway foreign object detection (RFOD) is critical to safe railway operation, yet scarce real positive samples incompletely represent task-relevant variations in object scale, intrusion relation, railway scene, illumination, and adverse weather. Existing synthetic augmentation can improve RFOD detection, but its gains lack an explicit account of the task-relevant deficiencies complemented by the generated data. We therefore introduce RailSyn, a diagnosis-guided framework comprising a real-referenced Inspector and a requirement-aligned Generator. The Inspector constructs a variable-radius empirical cover from finite real observations to localize candidate completion regions and profile synthetic pools. The resulting audit identifies railway-context, intrusion-semantic, and visual-consistency requirements; the Generator addresses them through domain adaptation, agent-planned placement and physical contact relations, and plan-consistent conditional refinement. Using the Inspector, we further trace representation-space changes across generation variants; the complete system attains a local-shell occupation of $C_{gap}$ to 13.64%, which measures generated coverage of real-derived completion regions. Extensive experiments show AP50--95 gains of up to 4.9 points and consistent improvements across nine mainstream detectors, demonstrating broad cross-architecture utility.

</details>


### [220] [SegWave: Wavelet-Driven Segmentation of Tampered Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30714)
*Siddhi Pravin Lipare,Vishesh Kumar,Akshay Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SegWave：结合Transformer与离散小波变换（DWT），并通过自适应子带注意力（ASA）强化高频子带，以更好地定位与检测图像篡改；在多基准上优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有取证方法多依赖高层视觉伪迹，将检测简化为二分类，难以在复杂场景中可靠验证图像真伪与篡改位置；需要一种能同时利用空间与频域、多尺度、可精细定位的方案。

Method: 构建混合框架SegWave：在空间域采用Transformer提取全局-局部特征；在频域引入DWT以获得多尺度子带表示；设计ASA模块对高频子带进行动态加权以突出与篡改相关的不一致性；最终进行像素级篡改定位与判别。

Result: 在多个公开基准与严苛评测设置下，SegWave在检测精度与篡改区域定位上均超越当前SOTA方法，表现稳定且鲁棒。

Conclusion: 空间-频域融合与自适应高频注意可显著提升图像篡改检测与定位效果；SegWave为复杂真实场景中的取证提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Verifying image authenticity is increasingly difficult, posing serious risks across journalism, law enforcement, and political domains. Most existing forensic methods rely on high-level visual artifacts and treat frame detection as a simple binary task. To address this, we propose SegWave, a hybrid framework that jointly leverages spatial and frequency-domain cues for image tampering detection. SegWave integrates a transformer-based architecture with the Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to capture localized, multi-scale frequency inconsistencies indicative of manipulation. To further improve localization effectiveness, we introduce an Adaptive Sub-band Attention module (ASA) that dynamically highlights the informative high-frequency wavelet components. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that SegWave consistently outperforms state-of-the-art tampering detection methods in challenging evaluation settings.

</details>


### [221] [RailGen: Improving Railway Intrusion Detection via Agent-Guided Small-Scale Foreign Object Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30727)
*Quan Hao,Ziyang Tao,Chenxi Zhang,Yudong Wang,Rui Shi,Liguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个用于长尾小目标检测的生成增强范式：用多模态生成器RailGen合成稀缺小目标样本，并配合改进的检测框架FocalDEIM，在RFOD任务上显著提升mAP与SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路异物入侵检测中，小目标易混淆且尾类样本稀缺，导致特征空间稀疏、类间边界模糊，现有检测器难以兼顾小目标辨识与长尾分布。

Method: 1) 构建多模态生成代理RailGen：在语义约束下自动调用工具生成铁路场景、标定入侵位置、提取异物并融合，得到逼真的小目标入侵合成图，从而密化尾类与小目标的特征表示；2) 提出检测框架FocalDEIM：在密集匹配中引入Focal Modulation提升小目标判别力，并采用Focal Loss强调困难样本，缓解复杂场景下的类间边界模糊；3) 用RailGen生成的高难度小目标样本对检测器进行增强训练。

Result: RailGen能生成高质量小尺度异物，目标像素面积最多缩小58倍，平均缩小13.85倍；在这些挑战样本的帮助下，相比基线DEIM，mAP@50提升5.6%，mAP@(50-95)提升7.5%，并超过现有SOTA；消融验证了特征空间增益与边界判别改进。

Conclusion: 多模态生成与改进检测联合，可有效缓解长尾小目标检测难题；RailGen补全小目标与尾类特征空间，FocalDEIM增强边界判别，整体范式在安全关键的铁路场景中取得显著收益。

Abstract: Small-object detection under long-tailed data distributions is a fundamental yet challenging problem in multimedia. Railway Foreign Object Detection (RFOD) epitomizes this challenge with easily confused small intrusions and scarce samples. To address these issues, we propose a generative-augmented detection paradigm that leverages multimodal image generation to enrich the feature space of rare and small objects. We first construct RailGen, a multimodal image generation agent based on large models. Under semantic constraints, RailGen automatically invokes tools to generate railway scenes, calibrate intrusion positions, extract foreign objects, and fuse them into realistic intrusion effects. This process produces high-quality synthetic samples that effectively densify the feature representations of tail classes and complete the small-object feature space. Within this paradigm, we further propose FocalDEIM, a detection framework designed to enhance training with generated data. FocalDEIM improves dense matching with Focal Modulation for better small-object discrimination and adopts Focal Loss to emphasize hard samples, thereby alleviating blurred inter-class boundaries in complex railway scenes. Experimental results demonstrate that RailGen can generate high-quality small-scale foreign objects, reducing the object pixel area by up to 58x and 13.85x on average. Equipped with these challenging samples, our paradigm surpasses the baseline DEIM by 5.6% and 7.5% in mAP@50 and mAP@(50-95), respectively, and outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. Ablation studies verify RailGen's feature-space enrichment and FocalDEIM's boundary discrimination. The paradigm provides an effective multimodal generative solution for long-tailed small-object detection in safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [222] [CheXGround: Anatomical Region Tokens for Grounded Longitudinal Chest X-ray Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30758)
*Adonay Demewez Gebremedhin,Wessam Shehieb,Sara Alansari,Mohamad Alansari,Muzammal Naseer,Sajid Javed,Naoufel Werghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CheXGround：一种将胸片序列按解剖区域对齐并落地到文字的多模态模型，显著提升纵向推理、临床文本质量与定位准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学多模态模型在报告生成、VQA与时间推理上已进步，但“纵向解读”与“视觉落地”多各自为政。如何用局部、可定位的证据来支撑跨时序比较仍缺乏系统方法。

Method: 从当前与既往胸片中自动提取解剖区域，形成成对ROI；将其编码为带时间信息的ROI token，并与全局时序图像上下文联合用于生成。提出Temporal Region–Phrase Alignment预训练目标，使时间增强的解剖表示与报告中的局部短语对齐；在多任务上训练并评估。

Result: 在单次与纵向VQA、纵向发现生成、时序可落地VQA、以及解剖定位等任务上，相比近期基线同时提升临床语言质量、时间推理能力与定位准确率。

Conclusion: 以解剖级别组织纵向证据是构建可落地的放射学语言模型的有效表示；CheXGround证明该思路能带来在临床相关任务上的全面增益。

Abstract: Recent radiology multi-modal language models have made substantial progress in chest X-ray report generation, visual question answering, and temporal reasoning. While longitudinal chest X-ray interpretation compares sequential examinations to describe change, visual grounding aims to connect clinical language with localized image evidence. Although longitudinal modeling and visual grounding have each advanced radiology language models, how localized visual evidence can support longitudinal interpretation remains under-explored. We introduce CheXGround, a region-grounded longitudinal chest X-ray language model that represents paired studies through corresponding anatomical regions. CheXGround extracts anatomical regions from current and prior radiographs, encodes them as temporally enhanced Region-of-Interest (ROI) tokens, and combines them with global temporal image context during generation. To connect these region tokens with clinical text, we propose Temporal Region--Phrase Alignment, a pretraining objective that aligns temporal anatomical representations with localized report phrases. We evaluate CheXGround on single-study and longitudinal Visual Question Answering (VQA), longitudinal findings generation, temporal grounded VQA, and anatomical grounding. Across these tasks, CheXGround improves clinical language quality, temporal reasoning, and localization accuracy over recent baselines. Our results suggest that organizing longitudinal evidence at the anatomical level is a strong representation for grounded radiology language modeling. Project page: https://adonaydem.github.io/chexground-website

</details>


### [223] [ChessQueries: Toward Better Chess Board Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30762)
*Joël Seytre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ChessQueries：ViT编码器+DETR风格解码器，实现棋盘格局识别SOTA；在ChessReD从15.3%跃升至99.2%，错误率每盘仅0.01格；具OOD鲁棒性，并发布更难的新公开数据集与代码模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有棋盘识别基准有限：ChessCog为合成数据，ChessReD来自单一手机拍摄场景，方法表现受限且泛化不足。需要一种在真实与分布外场景上都更准确稳健的方法与更具挑战的数据集来推动研究。

Method: 提出ChessQueries：采用视觉Transformer作为图像编码器，并用类似DETR的查询式解码器直接预测每个棋盘格的占用与棋子类别，实现端到端的检测/定位与分类联合建模。

Result: 在ChessReD基准上，将SOTA从15.3%提升到99.2%；在两数据集上几乎“饱和”任务，平均每盘仅0.01个方格预测错误（对比既有方法分别为3.4与0.15）。在分布外数据上也展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: ChessQueries在棋盘识别任务上显著超越现有方法并趋于饱和，展现出极强准确性与鲁棒性；作者还发布来自顶级赛事转播的更难公开数据集与SLCC数据、代码与权重以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Chess board recognition is the task of mapping the image of a chess board to the information of which piece is on which square. So far this task has two established benchmarks: ChessCog is synthetic, and ChessReD comes from smartphone pictures of a single chess board setup. We introduce ChessQueries, a new method combining a ViT encoder with a DETR-style decoder, which outperforms existing methods. On the ChessReD benchmark, we improve the state of the art from 15.3% to 99.2%, and demonstrate strong capabilities on out-of-distribution datasets. Our method saturates the task on the two datasets, with an average 0.01 wrong squares per board (vs. SotA: 3.4 / 0.15 respectively). We also share a new, harder public dataset, parsed from broadcasted top-level chess tournaments. Code, model weights and the SLCC data will be released.

</details>


### [224] [CORAL: A Benchmark for Structure-aware and Brain-wide Neuron Reconstruction in Light Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30768)
*Zekang Yang,Jiamin Li,Zhenghua Li,Jiaqi Fan,Zengcai Guo,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CORAL基准，用fMOST全脑数据评测神经元重建，含块级与全脑两任务，并引入结构感知指标与局部到全局追踪框架，比较主流方法，强调结构感知与稳健性的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动神经元重建方法在局部块上有进展，但缺乏能反映拓扑结构正确性且可扩展到全脑尺度的评测与方法，难以判断方法在实际全脑重建中的有效性。

Method: 1) 构建高质量全脑fMOST数据集与精标注；2) 设计两层级任务：块级重建与全脑重建；3) 提出基于纤维预测的结构感知评价指标，兼顾几何相似与拓扑正确性；4) 提出迭代式“局部到全局”的全脑追踪框架，可将任意局部重建方法扩展到全脑。

Result: 在CORAL上首次对主流局部重建方法进行了结构感知比较，并评估其在全脑重建中的表现；给出证据表明仅靠几何距离指标不足，结构感知评估更能揭示方法优劣；现有方法在全脑完整重建上仍不够稳健。

Conclusion: CORAL填补了结构感知、全脑尺度评测与方法的空白，凸显构建结构感知指标与提升算法稳健性的关键性，为推进可扩展、完整的神经元全脑重建提供基准与框架。

Abstract: Automatic neuron reconstruction from light microscopy images is a central problem in computational neuroanatomy. While recent methods have achieved encouraging results on local image blocks, it remains unclear whether such progress translates to reconstruction that is both structurally accurate and scalable to the whole-brain scale. We present CORAL, the first benchmark for structure-aware evaluation of automatic neuron reconstruction from light microscopy images at both local and whole-brain scales. Built on a high-quality whole-brain fMOST dataset with carefully curated annotations, CORAL establishes two progressive tasks: block-level reconstruction, which evaluates reconstruction methods under limited spatial context, and brain-wide reconstruction, which assesses complete neuron reconstruction at the whole-brain scale. To account for topological correctness beyond geometric distance similarity, we introduce a structure-aware metric based on fiber prediction. To further achieve complete neuron reconstruction across the entire brain, we develop a brain-wide neuron tracing framework that extends arbitrary local reconstruction methods to the whole-brain scale through an iterative local-to-global process. Using this benchmark, we provide the first structure-aware comparison of mainstream methods for local neuron reconstruction and further evaluate their performance in brain-wide reconstruction. Our results underscore the importance of structure-aware evaluation and the need for more robust methods for complete neuron reconstruction.

</details>


### [225] [PixelIR: Fidelity-Perception Decoupling via Pixel-Space Image-Residual Flow Matching for Efficient One-Step Real-World Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30782)
*Bingtian Qiao,Yue Shi,Yong Guo,Wenjun Zhang,Jiezhang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PixelIR：通过像素空间图像流与残差流解耦“保真-感知”，并蒸馏为单步学生，实现高效真实场景超分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Real-ISR常在同一网络内同时优化保真与感知，目标相互干扰且难以权衡；单步方法虽减少采样步数，但仍耦合优化并沿用昂贵高分辨率骨干。需要一种既缩短采样路径，又专门建模“忠实重建”和“感知细节”的方法，以提升效率与可控性。

Method: 提出PixelIR的保真-感知解耦框架：1) 基于像素空间的image flow matching，将退化观测直接映射到忠实重建；2) 基于残差的residual flow，从噪声合成缺失的感知细节，避免反复重学/覆盖完整重建；3) 采用金字塔粗到细结构，并将教师模型蒸馏为部署友好的单步学生，实现一次前向完成修复。

Result: 在RealSR与DRealSR上取得领先的PSNR、SSIM与LPIPS；最终模型单次评估即完成像素空间修复，参数32.9M、计算89.7G MACs、时延8.5ms。

Conclusion: 通过在像素空间将忠实重建与感知细节合成解耦，并蒸馏为单步学生，PixelIR在真实场景超分中实现了兼顾保真、感知与效率的强平衡，并具备实用部署价值。

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) aims to preserve structures supported by the degraded observation while reconstructing perceptually realistic details. However, existing Real-ISR methods largely optimize fidelity and perceptual quality within a shared network, causing the two objectives to interfere throughout training and making their balance difficult to control. Recent one-step methods reduce sampling steps, yet often inherit both this coupled optimization behavior and the expensive high-resolution backbone of their multi-step predecessors. We argue that efficient Real-ISR requires not only a shorter sampling trajectory, but also specialized modeling of faithful reconstruction and perceptual detail synthesis. Based on this insight, we propose PixelIR, a fidelity-perception decoupling framework built upon pixel-space image-residual flow matching. PixelIR first learns an image flow that maps the degraded observation to a faithful reconstruction. Then, a residual flow synthesizes the missing perceptual details from noise without repeatedly relearning or overwriting the complete restoration solution. We further distill the teacher into a deployment-oriented one-step student within a coarse-to-fine pyramid architecture. Extensive experiments show that PixelIR achieves leading PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS on both RealSR and DRealSR. The final model completes pixel-space restoration in a single evaluation with only 32.9M parameters, 89.7G MACs, and 8.5ms latency, demonstrating a strong practical fidelity-perception-efficiency balance.

</details>


### [226] [Camera trap classification with deep learning under ground truth uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30789)
*Leonard Hockerts,Peter S. Stewart,Sarthak Arora,Tiffany J. Vlaar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究探讨在深度学习训练中有意保留来自公民科学志愿者标注的不一致（标签不确定性）是否有助于生态相机捕获图像的分类性能。结果显示：适度的不确定性可提升总体准确率，尤其对志愿者认为困难的图像；物种级别准确率多有提高，但跨数据集泛化没有改善。ImageNet预训练放大了收益并减少训练轮数；在类不平衡下，整体准确率仍受益，但稀有类准确率下降，误分类更像志愿者的错误模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态图像数据庞大，专家标注昂贵，现实中常依赖志愿者众包。然而志愿者之间分歧导致“真值”不确定，传统做法通常强制采用单一共识标签，可能忽略不确定性的信息价值。作者欲检验：在训练中显式保留较高“真值不确定性”（如多标签/软标签）是否能提升模型对困难样本的鲁棒性与总体性能，并评估预训练与类不平衡的交互影响及跨数据集泛化。

Method: 使用两套含相机陷阱图像、同时具志愿者与专家分类的真实数据。构造不同水平的“真值不确定性”（例如利用志愿者分布作为软标签或保留分歧），对比标准单一标签训练。评估总体与物种级准确率、对困难样本的表现、跨数据集泛化。考察预训练方案：ImageNet预训练与在其他相机陷阱数据上的额外预训练；并分析类不平衡条件下的影响与误分类模式。

Result: 引入较高的标签不确定性训练可提高总体测试准确率，提升在志愿者困难图像上的表现；物种级准确率多有提升，但对外部数据集的泛化并未改善。ImageNet预训练显著增强上述收益并减少所需训练轮数；在相机陷阱数据上的进一步预训练降低计算成本但未带来额外准确率提升。在类不平衡下，总体准确率仍因不确定性而提高，但常见物种更准、稀有物种准确率下降；误分类模式更接近志愿者的错误。

Conclusion: 在生态相机图像分类中，适度保留志愿者分歧作为软标签可提升总体与困难样本的准确率；结合通用图像预训练效果更佳并更高效。然而，这并不改善跨数据集泛化，且在类不平衡下可能牺牲稀有类。实践上建议：在训练中纳入中等程度的标签分歧、采用ImageNet预训练，并关注类别不平衡的权衡与校正；在人机协同工作流中，可更有效地融合志愿者与模型的分类信息。

Abstract: Supervised deep learning methods enable the rapid processing of ecological image data, but depend on a costly annotation process. Consequently, training labels are commonly derived from volunteer citizen science projects. However, disagreement among volunteers introduces uncertainty in the "ground truth" data that are assumed to be correct for model training and validation. Using two datasets containing camera trap images with associated volunteer and expert classifications, we investigated the effects of training under higher ground truth uncertainty. We observed improved overall test accuracy, particularly for images that were more difficult for volunteers. Species-level accuracy also generally improved, but generalisation to a different dataset did not. The benefits of ground truth uncertainty were enhanced by pre-training on ImageNet. Pre-training also reduced the number of training epochs required; further reductions in computational cost, but not gains in accuracy, resulted from additional pre-training on other camera trap images. With unbalanced training data, we still observed a clear benefit of increased ground truth uncertainty for overall accuracy, especially on difficult images. Class imbalance improved accuracy for common species, reduced rare species accuracy, and changed patterns of misclassification to more closely resemble mistakes made by volunteers. Our findings have implications for applying deep learning across ecological image types with multiple labels. Practitioners can improve accuracy, especially on difficult examples, by including moderate levels of label disagreement during training and using models pre-trained on general image data. In addition to improving the use of citizen science-derived labels in model training, our study suggests avenues for more effectively integrating human and deep learning classifications in combined workflows. (abridged)

</details>


### [227] [A Composition-Aware Pretraining Framework for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30817)
*Aryan Kashyap Naveen,Abhishek Srinivas,Pranav Moothedath,Shrutilipi Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种“成分感知”的地理空间基础模型预训练框架，把每个像元映射为土地覆盖组成直方图，并用地球搬运距离作为优化目标，从而更好地捕捉卫星场景的组合性；在检索与场景分类上显著优于更大模型，并在细粒度任务上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间预训练多以单一概念/标签处理影像，忽视卫星场景往往是多种地物混合的组合属性，导致在需要区域级语义相似性判断的任务上表现受限。

Method: 构建“composition target”，即每个影像单元对应的分数土地覆盖直方图；将该直方图作为主监督信号，通过地球搬运距离（EMD）蒸馏进主干网络，实现成分感知预训练；使用36.8M参数主干并在多下游任务上评估。

Result: 相较SatMAE(303M)与Prithvi-EO-2.0(600M)，在零样本检索与场景分类多数设定中更优；在需要细粒度空间精度的分割与检测上保持竞争力；在ForestNet-12上mAP@10由0.279提升至0.434，相对提升55.6%。

Conclusion: 显式的土地覆盖组成建模能有效提升区域级理解与组合判别能力，以更小模型在多项任务上超越更大基线；证明组合感知预训练是地观基础模型的有力方向。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models have emerged as state-of-the-art methods for downstream Earth observation tasks. However, existing pretraining methodologies process imagery through a single-concept lens, failing to capture the highly compositional nature of complex satellite scenes. We propose a composition-aware pretraining framework that explicitly encodes fractional land-cover mixtures. Each satellite image cell is mapped to a histogram representing its fractional land-cover distribution, which we term the "composition target". These targets serve as the primary prediction objective and are distilled into the backbone using Earth Mover's Distance. Experimental evaluation shows that composition-aware pretraining yields substantial gains on region-level understanding tasks requiring semantic similarity judgment, including zero-shot image retrieval and scene classification, while remaining competitive on tasks requiring fine-grained spatial precision, such as segmentation and object detection. With a 36.8M-parameter backbone, our framework outperforms SatMAE and Prithvi-EO-2.0, which contain 303M and 600M parameters, respectively, in most retrieval and scene classification settings. On the fine-grained ForestNet-12 dataset, a rigorous testbed for compositional discrimination, our method boosts baseline mAP@10 from 0.279 to 0.434, a 55.6% relative improvement, providing direct evidence for the effectiveness of explicit composition modeling. The code implementation can be found at https://github.com/05kashyap/GFM_Composition_Pretraining

</details>


### [228] [RealOOB: A Definition-Consistent Real-World Oriented Occlusion Boundary Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30820)
*Lintao Xu,Yinghao Wang,Chenchu Rong,Xuchong Qiu,Chaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RealOOB数据集：包含426万像素级、定义一致的真实场景遮挡边界与有效性感知的遮挡朝向标注，覆盖物体间与自遮挡，旨在统一监督并评测方法在定位与朝向上的能力。评估显示定位上边缘检测器与OB方法相当，但朝向预测仍是普遍难点；强单目深度模型在真实OB处常缺乏可测几何不连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遮挡边界（OB）估计受限于碎片化监督：覆盖不足、类别特异、缺失自遮挡、定义不一致，导致难以系统训练与评测。同时，现代边缘与单目深度模型已很强，但其与“定义一致”的OB关系未被系统检验。

Method: 构建RealOOB真实数据基准：精细像素级OB标注，涵盖自遮挡与物体间遮挡；提供有效性感知的遮挡朝向图，仅在可可靠测量跨边界深度排序的像素上监督。基于该基准，系统评测40个OB/边缘检测器与6个单目深度估计器，在定位与朝向两方面比较。

Result: - 定位：现代边缘检测器在OB定位上与专门OB方法竞争力相当。
- 朝向：所有方法在遮挡朝向预测上表现不佳，存在明显推理缺口。
- 深度：即便是强单目深度估计器，在真实OB处常无法给出可测的几何与深度不连续。

Conclusion: RealOOB为OB估计提供了定义一致、几何扎实的基准与真实世界测试床，有助于统一评测并推动遮挡朝向推理与深度几何保真度研究；数据与代码将开源。

Abstract: Occlusion boundaries (OBs) are pixel-level image boundaries corresponding to surface visibility discontinuities caused by occlusion. Through precise boundary localisation and occlusion orientation, OBs encode local surface layout and depth ordering, providing geometry-driven mid-level cues for scene understanding. However, progress in pixel-level OB estimation has been limited by fragmented supervision: Existing benchmarks often suffer from limited coverage, category-specific designs, missing self-occlusion annotations, or inconsistent annotation definitions. Meanwhile, modern edge detectors and monocular depth estimators have become strong boundary and geometry predictors, yet their relationship to definition-consistent OBs remains underexplored. We introduce RealOOB, a carefully annotated real-world benchmark with 4.26M definition-consistent, geometry-grounded OB labels covering both inter-object and self-occlusion boundaries, together with validity-aware occlusion-orientation maps that restrict supervision to pixels whose cross-boundary depth ordering is reliably measurable. Based on RealOOB, we evaluate forty OB estimators and edge detectors alongside six monocular depth estimators. Our evaluation reveals a clear gap in occlusion reasoning: modern edge detectors perform competitively with OB methods in localisation, whereas orientation prediction remains challenging for all evaluated methods. Meanwhile, even strong depth estimators often fail to exhibit measurable geometry at true OBs. We believe RealOOB provides a strong reference benchmark for the OB estimation community and a real-world testbed for assessing depth discontinuities and geometry fidelity in broader low-level vision tasks. Dataset and code will be released.

</details>


### [229] [Lucida: Parse, Generate, and Place for Composable Real-to-Sim Scene Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30821)
*Minghan Qin,Yuang Wang,Xiuyu Yang,Yushi Long,Yujian Zhang,Ruihuan Wang,Kai Ye,Yangang Zhang,Hang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lucida 提出端到端的“可组合场景建模”管线：从视频解析多视实例证据→生成完整对象资产→通过VLM驱动的GUI操作完成精对齐；在检测、位姿、重建上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际室内环境采集常有遮挡、杂乱与不完整观测，使传统三步法在每步都依赖理想输入（准确几何、无遮挡视角、完美资产匹配）而失效；需要一种能以现实可得证据推进并将高精度推迟到末端收敛的方案。

Method: 1) 视频解析为场景图：节点携带每个实例的多视证据（外观、几何、视角聚合）。2) 资产生成：基于该证据为每个实例生成完整可编辑资产。3) 资产放置：提出 GizmoAct，将放置视为多轮GUI交互，由VLM策略闭环操控对象gizmo并自主判断对齐完成。

Result: 在三个任务上显著提升：R2S-Scene 的场景级3D检测 mAP 较 Boxer 提升 69%；CA-1M 上 ADD-SB@0.05 从 57.8% 提升到 83.4%；场景重建 F-Score 从 SAM3D 的 0.794 提升到 0.924。

Conclusion: 通过将精度需求后移并以多视证据与VLM驱动的闭环放置相结合，Lucida 在真实杂乱场景下实现更可靠的对象资产生成与场景复刻，提供可编辑、可操控的仿真就绪环境，并在多项基准上取得大幅领先。

Abstract: Composable scene modeling aims to recover a real indoor scene as complete, editable object assets arranged as observed, giving robot simulation and embodied AI a simulation-ready replica of the real environment whose objects can be manipulated individually. Existing pipelines decompose the task into three steps---parse the observations into instances, generate an asset for each, and place each asset back---but every step presumes an input that a cluttered capture rarely provides: accurate instance geometry, unoccluded views, and assets that accurately match the observations. We propose Lucida, which keeps this order but redistributes the requirements, so each step consumes only what a real capture reliably provides and precision is reached at the end of the pipeline rather than demanded at its start. Lucida parses the video into a scene graph whose nodes carry per-instance multi-view evidence, generates a complete asset for each instance from its evidence, and places assets with GizmoAct, a VLM policy that casts placement as multi-turn GUI interaction, manipulating the object's gizmo in a closed loop and deciding itself when alignment is reached. Across scene-level 3D object detection, object pose estimation, and scene reconstruction, Lucida improves mAP over Boxer by 69% on R2S-Scene, raises ADD-SB@0.05 from 57.8% to 83.4% on CA-1M, and increases scene F-Score from 0.794 for SAM3D to 0.924.

</details>


### [230] [Whole-Body MRI Classification via Prompt-Based Clinical Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30824)
*Laura Daza,Marta Hasny,Cristina González,Julia A. Schnabel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TACTIC：将WB-MRI与临床表格变量通过“临床属性提示（prompt）”进行条件化视觉特征学习，支持任意数量与缺失的临床输入，在多项全身MRI分类任务中优于仅图像基线并在缺失数据时保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态（影像+临床）可提升系统性疾病诊断，但传统融合方法要求固定且完整的临床输入，现实中表格变量常缺失或不齐，导致适用性受限。需要一种能灵活处理缺失与数量不定临床变量的融合方法。

Method: 提出TACTIC：将结构化临床属性编码为提示向量，作为条件信号注入到视觉Transformer中，进行条件化视觉特征学习。该设计无需预先定义固定输入结构，也不需对缺失变量填补；可在任意可用临床属性下运行。

Result: 在五个WB-MRI分类任务（糖尿病、COPD、乳腺癌、前列腺癌、转移）上，TACTIC在有临床信息时一致优于仅图像基线，并在临床输入不完整时仍保持强预测性能。

Conclusion: 基于提示的多模态融合为利用临床上下文改进WB-MRI分析提供了灵活且有效的方案；TACTIC在可变与缺失表格数据情景下展示了稳健性与性能收益，代码与模型权重已开源。

Abstract: Combining whole-body magnetic resonance imaging (WB-MRI) with clinical variables has the potential to improve systemic disease diagnosis by leveraging complementary sources of patient information. However, structured clinical variables are often incomplete or missing, limiting the applicability of conventional multimodal fusion methods that assume fixed inputs. In this work, we propose TACTIC (Tabular-Attribute Conditioned Transformer for Image Classification), a prompt-based multimodal framework that integrates WB-MRI and structured clinical data through conditional visual feature learning. By encoding clinical attributes as prompts, TACTIC supports an arbitrary number of tabular inputs and naturally handles missing data without requiring imputation or fixed input structures. We evaluate TACTIC on five WB-MRI classification tasks spanning systemic and oncologic applications, including diabetes, chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD), breast cancer, prostate cancer, and metastasis diagnosis. Across all tasks, TACTIC consistently improves performance over image-only baselines when clinical information is available while maintaining strong predictive capability under incomplete tabular inputs. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of prompt-based models as a flexible approach for improving WB-MRI analysis using clinical context. The model weights and code are available at https://github.com/lauradaza/TACTIC

</details>


### [231] [Reliable Benchmarking of Artifact Detection in Computational Pathology: A Reproducibility and Uncertainty Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30835)
*Konstantinos Moutselos,Ilias Maglogiannis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出并实证演示一套“可靠性协议”用于病理全视野图像(WSI)质量控制基准评测，量化并控制四类变异源，从而区分可复现实效与评测噪声；结果显示，现有小样本基准支撑的结论远弱于文献中常见表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前WSI质控方法的对比基准存在四个问题：独立切片数量很少；标注高度集中于少数切片；使用汇总体比度量（如pooled F1）却无闭式标准误；仅有单一继承的训练/测试划分。由此导致不同方法间的报告差异难以解释与不稳健。作者希望给出一套低成本、可复核的评测协议，揭示并量化评测中被忽视的不确定性来源。

Method: 提出“可靠性协议”以同时量化四类变异：测试集采样、训练随机性、划分构成、以及未记录的预处理。只有在四项检验下仍显著的效果才可报告。作者在独立重现实验中复现了已公开的基于扩散的伪影检测器，沿用原24张切片划分，并与一个监督式基线比较；评估包括对比学习辅助项的作用、不同设计变体差异，以及与基线的对比，并显式估计不确定性。

Result: 核心机制可复现：加入对比学习辅助项使pooled F1从0.673提升到0.688，并在第二随机种子下复现（+0.0156, p=0.031；+0.0190, p=0.005），但其主要改善的是“笔迹”而非论文动机中强调的其他伪影类型。比较性结论不可复现：不同设计变体之间及与监督基线的差异均落入评估不确定性内。数据方面，24张切片中仅4张贡献了70%的打分像素，有效样本量约6.2；继承的划分位于第7百分位。另发现未披露的组织区域过滤步骤：其排除了41.4%的失焦标注却仅排除2.6%的气泡标注，天生与模糊混淆。

Conclusion: 小队列WSI基准所能支撑的结论强度远低于当前报告暗示。所提四项检验计算成本低，适合伴随任何此类评估，用于区分可复现实效与评测无法分辨的差异。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Quality control is a prerequisite for whole-slide image analysis, yet the benchmarks on which quality-control methods are compared share four properties that make their reported differences hard to interpret: few independent slides, annotation concentrated in a minority of them, pooled ratio metrics with no closed-form standard error, and a single inherited train/test partition. We propose a reliability protocol for such benchmarks. Methods: The protocol quantifies four sources of variability - test-set sampling, training stochasticity, partition composition, and undocumented preprocessing - a claim is reportable only if it survives all four; three of the four cost minutes of compute. We apply it to an independent reconstruction of a published diffusion-based artifact detector, evaluated on the original 24-slide partition and against a supervised baseline. Results: The method's central mechanism reproduces: the auxiliary contrastive term improves pooled F1 from 0.673 to 0.688 and replicates under a second seed (+0.0156, p = 0.031; +0.0190, p = 0.005), although it acts on pen marking rather than the artifact types cited to motivate it. Its comparative claims do not: differences between design variants, and against the supervised baseline, fall inside the uncertainty of the evaluation. Four of 24 slides carry 70% of scored annotated pixels, giving an effective sample size of 6.2, and the inherited partition sits at the 7th percentile. An unreported tissue-restriction step excludes 41.4% of out-of-focus annotation against 2.6% of air bubble; such a gate is confounded with blur by construction. Conclusions: Small-cohort benchmarks support far weaker conclusions than current reporting implies. The four checks are cheap enough to accompany any evaluation on such a resource and separate reproducible effects from differences the evaluation cannot resolve.

</details>


### [232] [Physical Adversarial Examples for Person Detectors in Thermal Images Based on 3D Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30839)
*Xiaopei Zhu,Siyuan Huang,Zhanhao Hu,Jianmin Li,Jun Zhu,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于3D建模与气凝胶贴片的红外对抗服，能在真实环境下高效躲避热红外人体检测器（YOLOv9等），室内/室外成功率约80%/77%，显著优于随机贴片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外在自动驾驶、医疗等领域广泛应用，但其安全性与对抗鲁棒性研究不足。现有2D对抗贴片难以覆盖多视角、时空变化等真实世界因素，需一种更贴近真实成像与传热特性的物理对抗方案。

Method: 1) 基于真实红外照片构建红外3D人体/服装模型，并建立可随时间与位置变化的红外纹理映射；2) 在3D服装上进行对抗优化，求解若干黑色方块贴片（气凝胶在内侧形成冷斑，红外成像呈黑块）的布局与朝向；3) 物理实现：在衣物内侧特定位置贴气凝胶方块；4) 评估：对YOLOv9进行室内/室外攻击，并做随机贴片对照与跨检测器迁移（集成攻击）。

Result: 物理攻击对YOLOv9：室内80.11%成功率、室外76.85%；随机贴片仅26.53%/23.03%。对未知检测器也有较好迁移性（通过集成攻击验证）。

Conclusion: 3D建模驱动的红外对抗服能在真实多角度与时变条件下稳定欺骗热红外检测器，优于简单随机贴片，且具有跨模型迁移性，显示红外检测在实际系统中的安全隐患需重视。

Abstract: Thermal Infrared detection is widely used in autonomous driving, medical AI, etc., but its security has only attracted attention recently. We propose infrared adversarial clothing designed to evade thermal person detectors in real-world scenarios. The design of the adversarial clothing is based on 3D modeling, which makes it easier to simulate multiangle scenes near the real world compared to 2D modeling. We optimized the black patch layout pattern of 3D clothing based on the adversarial example technique and made physical adversarial clothing using the aerogel. The idea is to paste a set of square aerogel patches, which display black squares in thermal images, in the inner side of clothing at specific locations with specific orientations. To enhance realism, we propose a method to build infrared 3D models with real infrared photos and develop texture maps for 3D models to simulate varied infrared characteristics over time and location. In physical attacks, we achieved an attack success rate of 80.11\% indoors and 76.85\% outdoors against YOLOv9. In contrast, randomly placed patches yielded much lower success rates (26.53\% indoors and 23.03\% outdoors). The adversarial clothing also showed good transferability to unknown detectors with an ensemble attack method, demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [233] [Pretrained, Curriculum-Tuned, and Ensembled: A Tracer-Aware Interactive Segmentation Pipeline for AutoPET V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30844)
*Xinglong Liang,Chunyao Lu,Tianyu Zhang,Jiaju Huang,Tao Tan,Yunchao Yin,Lishan Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRIAGE：在全身PET/CT上进行示踪剂感知的交互式病灶分割，结合解剖上下文、示踪剂分支、两阶段分割与涂鸦细化，以适应FDG与PSMA差异并提升交互鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT的交互式病灶分割需要强初始预测与对稀疏涂鸦的高效响应；然而不同示踪剂（FDG、PSMA）在摄取模式、病灶外观和采集特性上差异大，易导致跨域泛化差、将生理摄取误判为病灶。需要一个同时感知示踪剂与解剖结构、可交互细化且具鲁棒性的框架。

Method: 提出TRIAGE框架：1) 主干为3D STU-Net，采用异步掩码策略的MAE预训练，学习可迁移的解剖与跨模态表示；2) 训练器官分割辅助模型，提供显式解剖上下文以区分生理与病理摄取；3) 示踪剂分类器将检查路由至FDG或PSMA分支；4) 每个分支内，第一阶段模型将CT、PET与器官上下文作为输入生成初始病灶掩膜；5) 将初始预测与前景/背景累计涂鸦结合，交由第二阶段交互细化网络迭代更新；6) 两分支共享流程但独立训练以建模示踪剂特异模式；7) 采用课程式训练与模型集成，增强跨交互步与异质数据的稳健性。

Result: 使用AutoPET V官方数据与十折划分进行实验；目前量化结果、消融与最终测试集表现尚未公布（占位符，待挑战评测后补充）。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过示踪剂感知路由、解剖上下文、两阶段交互细化与预训练，针对FDG/PSMA差异提供稳健的交互式病灶分割方案；预期提升初始预测质量与对稀疏涂鸦的响应，并增强跨队列鲁棒性。

Abstract: Interactive lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT requires a model to provide a strong initial prediction while also responding efficiently to sparse corrective scribbles during inference. This setting is particularly challenging because tracer distributions, physiological uptake patterns, lesion appearance, and acquisition characteristics differ substantially between FDG and PSMA studies. We present TRIAGE, Tracer-aware Refinement via Interactive Anatomy-Guided sEgmentation. The core backbone is a 3D STU-Net initialized through masked autoencoding pre-training with an asynchronous masking strategy, aiming to learn transferable anatomical and cross-modal representations before task-specific fine-tuning. In parallel, we train an auxiliary organ segmentation model whose predictions provide explicit anatomical context and help distinguish physiological uptake from malignant lesions. A dedicated tracer classifier first routes each study to an FDG- or PSMA-specific branch. Within each branch, a first-stage segmentation model consumes CT, PET, and organ context to generate an initial lesion mask. The initial prediction is then combined with cumulative foreground/background scribbles and refined by a second interactive segmentation network. The FDG and PSMA branches share the same overall processing pipeline but are trained independently to account for tracer-specific appearance and error modes. We additionally employ curriculum-style training and model ensembling to improve robustness across interaction steps and heterogeneous cohorts. Experiments are conducted using the official AutoPET V data and ten-fold split; quantitative results, ablations, and final test-set performance are left as placeholders to be completed after the challenge evaluation. Code: https://github.com/Liiiii2101/AUTOPET2026-MEDAI.

</details>


### [234] [TAMI: Temporally Aligned, Missingness-Aware, and Interpretable Multimodal Fusion for Mental Health Assessment in Older Adults with Mild Cognitive Impairment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30857)
*Merna Bibars,Bolaji Omofojoye,Allan I. Levey,Rachel Hershenberg,Gari D. Clifford,Hyeokhyen Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出TAMI框架，以时间对齐、缺失感知与可解释融合来改进远程多模态访谈中老年MCI人群抑郁与焦虑筛查，AUROC约0.68–0.69；时间对齐带来最大增益；抑郁更多依赖眼动与开放式问题，焦虑依赖眼动与头部姿态；仅用开放式问题也能接近全程表现，支持以开放式问题为中心的访谈设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程临床访谈是可扩展的筛查方式，但现有多模态方法存在三大痛点：跨模态特征时间分辨率不一致导致伪相关；远程记录经常有模态掉线与缺失，零填充混淆缺失与真实近零值；缺乏多层次可解释性，难以定位到模态、题目与时刻的贡献，限制临床解读与协议优化。

Method: 提出TAMI（Temporally-Aligned, Missingness-Aware, Interpretable）多模态融合框架：1) 在问答段内将语音、语言、面部、生理特征对齐到共享时间轴，细粒度时间对齐；2) 显式编码随时间变化的模态级缺失（missingness-aware），避免将缺失当作零值；3) 融合时条件化于问题上下文，实现对模态、问题与时刻的联合归因。基于49名MCI老年人的远程访谈数据进行实验与解释性分析。

Result: TAMI在抑郁与焦虑分类上分别达到AUROC 0.68与0.69；引入细粒度时间对齐带来最大性能提升（Δ≥0.1）。多层可解释性显示：抑郁判别主要依赖眼动和开放式问题；焦虑判别更依赖眼动与头部姿态，且归因在各问题上较均匀。仅用开放式问题回答（约5.1分钟）训练的抑郁模型AUROC为0.67，与使用完整19分钟访谈无显著差异（p>0.05）。

Conclusion: 时间对齐与缺失感知是远程多模态心理筛查的关键；TAMI在小样本MCI老年群体中取得稳健性能并提供多层可解释性。开放式问题在抑郁筛查中信息密度高，可据此优化访谈协议以缩短时长、提升可及性。

Abstract: Depression and anxiety in older adults with Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) are frequently underdiagnosed due to limited access to care. Multimodal analysis of remote clinical interviews is a scalable screening approach, but existing methods have three limitations. First, they do not correct temporal misalignment across multimodal features extracted at different resolutions, inducing spurious cross-modal associations. Second, remote recordings exhibit uneven modality dropout, but missing values are often zero-filled, making them indistinguishable from valid near-zero measurements. Finally, they do not jointly attribute predictions to modalities, questions, and interview moments, limiting fine-grained clinical interpretation. We propose a Temporally-Aligned, Missingness-Aware, Interpretable (TAMI) multimodal fusion framework. TAMI aligns speech, language, facial, and physiological features within question-answer segments on a shared timeline, encodes modality-level missingness over time, and conditions fusion on question context. In interviews with 49 older adults with MCI, TAMI achieved area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) scores of 0.68 (depression) and 0.69 (anxiety). Fine-grained temporal alignment of multimodal features produced the largest performance gain ($Δ{\geq}0.1$). Multi-level interpretability analysis revealed that depression classification relied on eyegaze and open-ended questions, while anxiety classification depended on eyegaze and head pose, with attribution uniformly distributed across questions. Using only responses to the open-ended questions (5.1min), the depression model achieved an AUROC score of 0.67, which was not significantly different from using the full interview (19min) ($p>0.05$). Our findings support designing interview protocols centered on open-ended questions for depression screening in older adults with MCI.

</details>


### [235] [VCAR: Training-Free 3DGS Segmentation via View Completeness and Axis-Aware Boundary Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30870)
*Kun Cao,Di Wang,Haibin Zhu,Haozhi Huang,Xu Wang,Zheng Shi,Guanghua Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VCAR提出一种无需训练的3DGS语义分割粗到细流程：基于可见性加权的多视角投票快速定位目标，再通过球面螺旋采样生成补充视角进行精细边界投票，并引入轴感知边界细化按主导轴定向压缩各向异性高斯，显著提升边界质量与效率，在NVOS与LERF上达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS语义分割多依赖蒸馏到场景的特征，训练成本高且易产生边界模糊。作者观察到边界伪影来自视角覆盖不足与各向异性高斯在边界处的溢出，因此希望在不训练的前提下提升分割精度与边界清晰度。

Method: 提出VCAR：1) 粗阶段：利用可见性权重的多视角投票快速确定目标区域；2) 细阶段：以粗分割为中心构建物体球体，用球面螺旋采样(SSS)生成补充视点，对增强视图再次投票以抑制无关高斯并精化边界；3) 轴感知边界细化(ABR)：将投影2D协方差分解到各轴，识别导致泄漏的主导轴，仅沿该轴进行各向异性压缩，减少边界外溢。无需额外训练。

Result: 在NVOS与LERF基准上实现最优或同类最佳的分割精度与效率，边界更清晰，显著降低每场景开销。

Conclusion: 通过视角完备性驱动的多视图投票与轴向选择性的各向异性压缩，VCAR在无需训练的前提下有效缓解3DGS分割的边界伪影并提升准确率与效率，适用于场景级快速三维理解。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is crucial for advancing 3D scene understanding. Existing methods predominantly rely on feature distillation, which incurs substantial per-scene training overhead and often yields blurred segmentation boundaries. We identify that these boundary artifacts are driven in part by insufficient viewpoint coverage and boundary overflow of anisotropic Gaussian primitives. To address these challenges, we propose VCAR, a training-free coarse-to-fine segmentation strategy based on View Completeness and Axis-aware Boundary Refinement. In the coarse stage, a visibility-based weighted multi-view voting scheme rapidly localizes the target. In the fine stage, an object-centric sphere derived from the coarse result generates supplementary viewpoints via Spherical Spiral Sampling (SSS), allowing multi-view voting on the augmented views to precisely refine object boundaries and suppress irrelevant 3D Gaussians. Moreover, we introduce Axis-aware Boundary Refinement (ABR) to mitigate artifacts from anisotropic primitives. By decomposing the projected 2D covariance into per-axis contributions, ABR identifies the dominant axis responsible for boundary leakage and applies targeted anisotropic compression exclusively along that axis. Extensive experiments on NVOS and LERF demonstrate that VCAR achieves state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy and efficiency without training. Our code is available at https://github.com/DDKK0526/VCAR.

</details>


### [236] [SurgSkill-Bench: A Benchmark for Multimodal Surgical Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30872)
*Chaohui Dang,Zheheng Jiang,James Glasbey,David Luke,Theodoros Arvanitis,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SurgSkill-Bench：含214段手术训练视频、六维OSATS评分与专家评论的基准数据集；定义视频仅预测与评论辅助两种评估；基线显示内容自适应采样与视频-文本协同注意力有效，AUROC最高0.88；并讨论小规模与元数据不全等限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客观评估外科技术技能对培训与反馈至关重要，但当前依赖专家人工评审，成本高、效率低。已有自动方法多聚焦视觉信号，缺乏将操作表现、结构化评分及评审文本联合研究的框架与数据。为填补这一空白，作者构建一个视频-评分-文本结合的基准，以系统比较与推动自动技能评估研究。

Method: 构建SurgSkill-Bench数据集：214段手术模拟训练视频，配套六维OSATS评分与专家自由文本评论。设定两种评估设置：（1）仅视频输入进行OSATS二分类预测（以数据集内各维度中位数为阈值二分）；（2）事后“专家评论辅助”预测，将评审文本作为辅助信号。基线采用：若干冻结视觉主干；内容自适应关键帧采样；以及简单的视频-文本共注意力融合模块。在内部视频级验证下进行对比。

Result: 在仅视频设定下，内容自适应采样优于简单采样；在评论辅助设定下，专家文本提供额外与评分相关的信号，提升预测表现。在以数据集特定中位数二分的评估下，最佳平均AUROC达到0.88。

Conclusion: SurgSkill-Bench为联合视频、结构化评分与专家文本的外科技能评估提供了初步基准。基线结果表明内容自适应采样与视频-文本融合有效，但受限于数据规模、元数据完整性及“评论辅助预测”的解释性问题。未来需扩充数据、完善标注与更严谨的评估；代码将后续公开。

Abstract: Objective assessment of surgical technical skill is important for surgical training and structured feedback, but current workflows remain dependent on labor-intensive expert review. Existing automated approaches primarily focus on visual inputs and provide limited support for jointly studying operative performance, structured skill scores, and evaluator feedback. We introduce SurgSkill-Bench, an initial video-score-text benchmark-style dataset containing 214 surgical training simulation videos, six-dimensional OSATS scores, and expert free-text comments. We define two evaluation settings: video-only OSATS prediction for automated assessment and post hoc expert-comment-assisted prediction, where evaluator comments are available as auxiliary information. We provide controlled baseline experiments using representative frozen visual backbones, content-adaptive key-frame sampling, and a simple video-text co-attention fusion module. Under internal video-level validation, content-adaptive sampling improves video-only performance in this dataset, while evaluator comments provide additional score-related signal in the assisted setting. The best mean AUROC reaches 0.88 under dataset-specific median dichotomization. We further discuss evaluation constraints related to dataset scale, metadata completeness, and the interpretation of comment-assisted prediction. Code will be released publicly at a later date.

</details>


### [237] [A Controlled Evaluation of Model Rankings and Input Reliance in Surface Water Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30895)
*Kittipat Phunjanna,Kristóf Karacs,Chayut Ngamkhanong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文评估水面分割常用全局IoU等汇总指标来排序模型，但这种排序无法解释“为何更好”“排序是否稳健”“对哪些输入依赖”。本文用多种针对性实验揭示排名稳定性、组件归因与输入依赖需要独立证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行业普遍用单一聚合指标（如全局IoU）给模型配置排名，但这无法支撑更细的结论：性能差异原因、排名在不同随机种子/地域分布下是否稳定、模型是否依赖特定输入（地形、土地覆被等），以及结论能否外推到不同部署情境。

Method: 在Sen1Floods11上进行：1) 多次配置对比与三种子重复实验；2) 成对测试芯片（paired test-chip）分析；3) 在固定检查点（checkpoint）下对输入进行“压力测试”（剔除/扰动特定辅助输入，如DEM、WorldCover）；4) 地理重加权评估不同区域对排名的影响。并在GEOID-Flood上对监督式辅助输入配置做次级评估，比较Swin-UNet与U-Net及跨模态学生（cross-modal student）。

Result: 跨模态学生在Sen1Floods11上获得最高的三种子平均IoU，但近似接近的排名会随随机种子与地理加权而变化；且不同架构（Swin-UNet vs U-Net）对辅助输入（如地形、WorldCover）的排名偏好不一致。GEOID-Flood上，监督式辅助输入的效应总体一致，但具体架构的优劣仍依赖配置。固定检查点压力测试显示模型对地形与WorldCover有显著依赖，却未能证明这些“干净输入”能提升纯净条件下的性能。由于目标语义与WorldCover的先验，评价实质受限为回溯性的“全水体”分割。

Conclusion: 汇总指标适合给“完整配置”做总体排名，但若要声称排名稳健性、组件贡献、输入依赖或可部署范围，需提供与之匹配的独立证据。评估与结论必须“证据对齐”：用什么证据，只能支撑什么类型的主张。

Abstract: Performance evaluation for surface-water segmentation commonly uses an aggregate metric such as global intersection-over-union (IoU) to rank model configurations. However, a configuration ranking does not by itself establish why one system performs better, whether a close ordering is stable, or how strongly predictions rely on individual inputs. We examine these distinctions primarily on Sen1Floods11 through repeated configuration comparisons, paired test-chip analysis, fixed-checkpoint input stress tests, and geographic reweighting, with a targeted secondary evaluation of supervised input configurations on GEOID-Flood. The cross-modal student achieves the highest three-seed mean IoU on Sen1Floods11, but close orderings vary across seeds and geographic weighting, while ancillary-input rankings differ between Swin-UNet and U-Net. The GEOID-Flood evaluation shows substantial agreement in supervised ancillary-input effects, although the exact architecture ordering remains configuration dependent. Fixed-checkpoint tests further establish reliance on terrain and WorldCover without establishing a clean-input performance benefit, while target semantics and the later WorldCover prior restrict the evaluation to retrospective all-water segmentation. These results show that aggregate metrics remain useful for ranking complete configurations, but ranking stability, component attribution, input reliance, and deployment scope require distinct evidence. Performance evaluation should therefore match the evidence reported to the claim being made.

</details>


### [238] [Rad-R: A Raw-ADC Radar Dataset and Capture-Invariant SSM for Hardware-Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30896)
*Mainak Mallick,Junghwan Yim,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发布Rad-R：首个系统化标注硬件故障严重度的汽车毫米波雷达原始ADC数据集，并提出原始IQ序列建模的RadrNet，显著提升跨严重度与anytime、few-shot场景的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中的汽车毫米波雷达会出现震动、天线失准、罩盖遮挡、接收通道退化等硬件故障，直接污染感知前端信号。然而获取这类故障数据昂贵且稀缺，现有原始雷达数据集缺少受控且可校准的故障标注与跨模态同步，导致对模型鲁棒性与泛化（尤其跨故障严重度）研究受限。

Method: 构建Rad-R数据集：使用TI 77GHz 4芯片级联（192虚拟通道）采集单会话数据，针对多种硬件故障设置可控严重度，并提供独立的物理严重度测量；同时帧同步IMU、温度、GPS、相机。提出原始IQ序列状态空间模型RadrNet（Mamba SSM变体），设计多个评测协议：片内（within-clip）、按chirp随时预测（anytime）、少样本跨采集（few-shot cross-capture）、以及受控跨严重度（train/test为物理上不同采集）。并与7个代表性视觉骨干和RD-CNN进行可复现实验对比。

Result: - 片内性能接近饱和（macro-F1 > 0.98）。- 跨严重度泛化困难：使用绝对相位的RadrNet-DS在受控跨严重度上降至0.49 macro-F1。- 用每帧标准化幅度与相对chirp间相位替代绝对相位的RadrNet-DS-CI在受控基准上排名第一（macro-F1 0.663 vs. 最强RD-CNN 0.628，3种随机种子）；RadrNet系列在anytime和few-shot预算下也领先。- 跨模态分析显示：雷达微多普勒与IMU振动能量显著协变（条件汇总Spearman ρ=0.41）。

Conclusion: Rad-R为研究雷达前端硬件故障感知与鲁棒学习提供了首个带受控严重度与跨模态同步的原始ADC基准。绝对相位对跨严重度泛化不利，使用幅度标准化与相对相位可显著提升稳健性。数据集与代码将以宽松许可公开，便于复现实验与后续研究。

Abstract: Automotive mmWave radar can develop vibration, antenna misalignment, radome blockage, and receive-channel degradation that corrupt the signal before perception begins. Data for these faults are scarce because each condition must be induced and measured on physical hardware. We introduce Rad-R, a raw-ADC dataset captured with a 4-chip 77GHz TI MMWCAS-RF-EVM cascade (192 virtual channels). Unlike existing raw-radar datasets, Rad-R pairs each recording with a controlled hardware fault at a calibrated severity, an independent physical severity measurement, and frame-synchronised IMU, temperature, GPS, and camera streams. Rad-R is a single-session dataset, so our generalisation claims are confined to a controlled cross-severity protocol in which train and test use physically distinct captures. A reproducible benchmark evaluates seven representative vision backbones and the proposed raw-IQ Mamba SSM (RadrNet) under within-clip, chirp-wise anytime, few-shot cross-capture, and controlled cross-severity protocols. Within-clip performance is near-saturated ($>0.98$ macro-F1), whereas cross-severity generalisation remains difficult: the absolute-phase RadrNet-DS falls to $0.49$ macro-F1. RadrNet-DS-CI replaces absolute phase with per-frame-standardised magnitude and relative chirp-to-chirp phase and ranks first on the controlled benchmark ($0.663$ vs. $0.628$ for the strongest RD-CNN; three seeds); the RadrNet family also leads on the anytime and few-shot budgets. A descriptive cross-modal analysis further finds that radar micro-Doppler covaries with independently measured IMU vibration energy (pooled Spearman $ρ=0.41$ across conditions). The complete dataset and code will be released publicly under permissive licences.

</details>


### [239] [Uncertainty-Aware Trajectory Forecasting from Imperfect Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30899)
*Stephane Da Silva Martins,Victor Petrovic,Emanuel Aldea,Sylvie Le Hégarat-Mascle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种将跟踪不确定性显式传递给轨迹预测器的插件式不确定性感知框架：用高斯分布表示观测与预测，并通过总方差定律整合定位与关联不确定性，结合OU时序噪声与蒸馏训练，提升位移精度与概率预测的可靠-锐利权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中的轨迹来自不完美的多目标跟踪器，存在抖动、漏检、关联歧义等噪声；而主流预测模型通常在“干净”轨迹上训练与评测，失配导致部署性能下降。作者希望把跟踪产生的不确定性当作有用信号，而非简单去噪丢弃，从而获得对观测噪声鲁棒且校准良好的预测。

Method: 1) 不确定态表示：将每个观测状态编码为高斯分布，其协方差由检测层面的定位不确定性与关联层面的歧义通过总方差定律融合。2) 模型适配：最小改动地把输入轨迹作为高斯观测喂入现有骨干，并输出高斯分布形式的预测轨迹（而非点估计）。3) 训练策略：引入时间相关的Ornstein–Uhlenbeck扰动，模拟结构化观测噪声；并用基于响应的知识蒸馏，从在干净轨迹上训练的教师模型向学生传递监督信号。

Result: 在Oxford Town Centre与VIRAT上使用真实跟踪器输出评测，并在ETH/UCY上通过伪检测协议补充实验；相比基线，方法提升位移误差指标，并在概率预测上实现更好的可靠性-锐利度权衡（校准度更高且分布更集中）。

Conclusion: 把跟踪不确定性以高斯形式端到端传递给轨迹预测器，并用时序噪声建模与蒸馏联合训练，可在真实噪声环境下提高轨迹预测的准确性与概率校准，且对现有模型架构改动最小、可即插即用。

Abstract: Most trajectory forecasting models are trained on clean annotated histories, and are often evaluated under the same idealized assumption, although practical deployments rely on trajectories produced by imperfect multi-object trackers. The real-world observations exhibit localization jitter, missed or unstable detections, and data-association ambiguity, which are usually either ignored or removed through denoising. This paper instead treats tracking-derived reliability cues as an informative signal to be propagated to the predictor. We propose a plug-in uncertainty-aware formulation in which each observed state is encoded as an uncertain state representation, modeled by a Gaussian distribution whose covariance combines detection-level localization uncertainty and association-level ambiguity through the law of total variance. Existing backbones are adapted with minimal architectural changes: input trajectories are represented as Gaussian observations, and predicted trajectories are produced as Gaussian forecasts rather than deterministic coordinates. To train predictors that remain robust under structured observation noise, we combine temporally correlated Ornstein-Uhlenbeck perturbations with response-based knowledge distillation from a teacher trained on clean trajectories. Experiments on Oxford Town Centre and VIRAT using real tracker outputs, together with a complementary ETH/UCY pseudo-detection protocol, show that the proposed formulation improves displacement accuracy and the reliability-sharpness trade-off of probabilistic forecasts.

</details>


### [240] [Audio-Driven Adversarial Defense for 3D Talking Face Generation with totally Visual Fidelity Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30951)
*Rui-Qing Sun,Chen-Hao Cui,Hui-Yang Zhao,Tian Lan,Zhijing Wu,Xian-Ling Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种不可感知的音频端防护，通过心理声学掩蔽在语音被掩蔽频带注入微扰，显著削弱音频驱动的3D说话人脸生成，同时基本不影响人类主观音质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉域对抗扰动会破坏人脸视觉质量、易被缩放等变换削弱，且人脸具有强结构先验与社会敏感性；需要一种既能保护身份又不牺牲视觉可用性的主动防御。

Method: 将防护转移到音频模态：依据心理声学掩蔽模型，定位语音中感知掩蔽频带，在这些频带内叠加精心设计的微扰，抑制语音到3D人脸驱动映射的稳定性，从而干扰可靠的面部动画生成；扰动设计兼顾掩蔽阈值与生成模型敏感性，以最小可感知失真实现最大退化效果。

Result: 大量实验显示：在多种音频驱动3D说话人脸系统上，生成质量与同步性显著下降（面部动画被破坏/不稳定），而客观与主观音质评价保持良好；方法对常见真实世界变换更稳健。

Conclusion: 心理声学引导的音频微扰是保护音视频人像隐私的可行且有效方向，可在不破坏视觉呈现的前提下显著削弱可复用3D人像与口型驱动能力。

Abstract: The rapid development of generative portrait models has raised growing concerns about privacy leakage and identity misuse. In particular, audio-driven 3D talking face generation can reconstruct a reusable 3D portrait of a target person from a monocular video and animate it with arbitrary speech, making realistic identity impersonation alarmingly practical. Existing proactive defenses mainly operate in the visual domain by injecting subtle perturbations into acial regions to disrupt identity acquisition. However, such perturbations often compromise visual quality due to the strong structural priors and social sensitivity of human faces, and are easily weakened by common real-world transformations such as resizing. To overcome these limitations, we propose an imperceptible audio defense for audio-driven 3D talking face generation by shifting protection from the visual modality to the audio modality. Specifically,we exploit psychoacoustic masking to hide protective perturbations within perceptually masked frequency regions of the speech signal, thereby reducing perceptual distortion while suppressing reliable facial animation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method effectively degrades 3D talking face generation while preserving favorable perceptual quality. These findings highlight psychoacoustically guided audio perturbations as a practical and promising direction for privacy-preserving portrait protection.

</details>


### [241] [LOCI: A Locator-Critic with Refinement Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30959)
*Walid Bousselham,Mathilde Caron,Arsha Nagrani,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出LOCI，一个无需训练的“定位-批判”框架，通过先找证据再验证证据，显著提升VLM在复杂视觉任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在需要复杂视觉理解时往往答非所问。作者认为根因不是高层推理不足，而是感知落地失败：模型没有正确找到图像中的关键细节，导致推理合理但基于错误证据。

Method: 提出Locator-Critic（LOCI）框架并解耦两阶段：1）Locator代理进行视觉搜索，提出候选证据区域/片段；2）Critic代理评估这些证据对问题的相关性与充分性；两者进行迭代细化，持续改进证据，直到足以回答问题。整个流程“训练免”，可直接包裹不同VLM。

Result: 在多个复杂视觉基准上达到SOTA：对开源模型如Qwen3-VL，V*提高+12.1、HR-Bench+5.8、VisualProbe-Hard+11.2；对商用模型如Gemini 2.5 Pro，V*+8.9、HR-Bench+4.3、VisualProbe-Hard+4.8。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉定位与证据评估解耦并引入自我纠错循环，LOCI显著缓解VLM的“错位感知”问题，能以零训练开销泛化到多模型与数据集上，带来稳定准确率提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) still struggle on tasks requiring complex visual understanding. We argue that the core issue is not high-level reasoning, but instead failing to locate critical details in the image. Due to this shortcoming, VLMs generate often plausible but incorrect reasoning based on flawed perceptual grounding. To address this, we propose Locator-Critic (LOCI), a training-free framework that decouples visual search from evidence verification. LOCI employs a Locator agent to propose candidate visual evidence and a separate Critic agent to evaluate its relevance and sufficiency. These agents engage in an iterative refinement loop, progressively improving the evidence until it is adequate to answer the given question.
